  administration, set the fees to be paid to it for those services:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} services related to a Canadian maritime document;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} services related to any approvals or
    certifications;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the conduct or witnessing of tests; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} services specified in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not public money

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise provided in the regulations, the
  fees referred to in subsection (1) are not public money within the
  meaning of the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) and the
  [Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4) does not apply in respect of them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of certain regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise provided in the regulations, the
  regulations made under paragraph 35(1)(g) do not apply in respect of
  any service referred to in any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (d) that is
  provided by any person or organization that is not part of the federal
  public administration in the exercise of powers or the performance of
  duties under this Act in accordance with an agreement or arrangement
  entered into by the Minister of Transport under paragraph 10(1)(c).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 31, s. 161
- 2017, c. 20, s. 454
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-36.1-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 36.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51273 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of section 23

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
contravenes section 23 (destruction of documents, fraud, obstruction,
false or misleading information or statement, movement of detained
vessel) commits an offence and is liable on summary conviction to a fine
of not more than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more
than 18 months, or to both.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of regulations made under
paragraph 35(1)(d) or (3)(a)

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person who, or vessel or oil handling facility that, contravenes a
  provision of the regulations made under paragraph 35(1)(d) or (3)(a)
  commits an offence and is liable on summary conviction to a fine of
  not more than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more
  than 18 months, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a court sentencing a person, vessel or oil
  handling facility under subsection (1) for contravening a provision of
  the regulations made under paragraph 35(1)(d) or (3)(a) is of the
  opinion that the provision that the person, vessel or facility
  contravened is equivalent to a provision of the regulations made under
  another provision of this Act and if the punishment provided under
  this Act for contravening that provision of the regulations is less
  than the punishment provided under subsection (1), the person, vessel
  or oil handling facility is liable to that lesser punishment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person commits an offence who contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 17(2) (improper possession of a Canadian
    maritime document); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 28(7) (inform Chair without delay).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than one
  year, or to both.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person who, or vessel that, contravenes any of the following commits
  an offence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 16(3) (cheating on an exam);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 18 (failure to produce Canadian maritime
    document);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 20(2) (failure to return suspended or
    cancelled Canadian maritime document); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under paragraph
    35(1)(e) or (3)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that, commits an offence
  under subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not
  more than \$25,000.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 40
- 2017, c. 26, s. 40
- [2023, c. 26, s. 365](#1410308-1413745){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410308-1413745.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-40-20171212.html "Link to previous version of section 40"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of interim order or
regulations

- **[[40.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that,
  contravenes any of the following commits an offence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 10.1(4) (compliance with interim order);
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a provision of a regulation made under subsection
    35.1(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that, commits an offence
  under subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not
  more than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than
  18 months, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 27, s. 693
:::

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Registration, Listing and Recording]{.HTitleText1} {#h-51308 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51309 .Subheading}

Definition of [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Transport.

### [Canadian Register of Vessels and Registrars]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51312 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of Chief Registrar

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** An officer, to be known
as the Chief Registrar, is to be appointed or deployed under the [Public
Service Employment Act](/eng/acts/P-33.01).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties and powers of Chief Registrar

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Chief Registrar is responsible for establishing and maintaining a
  register to be known as the Canadian Register of Vessels. The Chief
  Registrar is to divide the Register into parts, including a small
  vessel register, for the classes of vessels that the Chief Registrar
  specifies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Register is to contain records of the information
  and documents specified by the Chief Registrar in respect of a
  Canadian vessel or a fleet that is registered under this Part,
  including its description, its official number, the name and address
  of its owner and, in the case of a vessel that is not registered in
  the small vessel register, details of all mortgages registered in
  respect of it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 43
- 2011, c. 15, s. 38
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-43-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 43"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registrars

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Chief Registrar may appoint the registrars that the Chief Registrar
  considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of registrars

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A registrar is to perform the duties and fulfil the
  responsibilities that the Chief Registrar assigns to the registrar.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** The Chief Registrar and
the registrars are not personally liable for anything they do or omit to
do in good faith under this Act.

### [Registration, Listing and Recording]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51329 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory registration of vessels

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  it is exempted under the regulations, a vessel must be registered
  under this Part if it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not a pleasure craft;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is wholly owned by qualified persons; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is not registered, listed or otherwise recorded in
    a foreign state.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Owner's obligation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every owner of a vessel that is required by
  subsection (1) to be registered under this Part shall ensure that it
  is so registered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory registration --- government
  vessels

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every government vessel must be registered under this
  Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 46
- 2011, c. 15, s. 39
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 46"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional registration

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** Unless they are
registered, listed or otherwise recorded in a foreign state, the
following vessels may be registered under this Part:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a pleasure craft that is wholly owned by qualified
  persons;

- [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} a vessel that is exempted under the regulations
  from the registration requirement in subsection 46(1) and that is
  wholly owned by qualified persons;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vessel that is owned by a corporation incorporated
  under the laws of a foreign state if one of the following is acting
  with respect to all matters relating to the vessel, namely,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a subsidiary of the corporation incorporated under
    the laws of Canada or a province,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an employee or a director in Canada of a branch
    office of the corporation that is carrying on business in Canada, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a ship management company incorporated under the
    laws of Canada or a province; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a vessel that is in the exclusive possession of a
  qualified person under a financing agreement under which the person
  will acquire ownership on completion of the agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 47
- 2011, c. 15, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bare-boat chartered vessels

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** A vessel that is
registered in a foreign state and that is bare-boat chartered
exclusively to a qualified person may be listed under this Part as a
bare-boat chartered vessel for the duration of the charter if, for the
duration of the charter, the registration is suspended in respect of the
right to fly the flag of that state.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vessels under construction

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** A vessel that is about to
be built or that is under construction in Canada may be temporarily
recorded in the Register as a vessel being built in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vessels built outside Canada

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding sections
46 to 48, the Minister may direct the Chief Registrar to refuse to
register or list a vessel built outside Canada.

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51359 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for the registration, listing or recording of a vessel
  must be made in the form and manner, include the information and be
  accompanied by the documents specified by the Chief Registrar.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further evidence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the specified information and
  documents, the Chief Registrar may require an applicant to provide
  evidence, including declarations, that the Chief Registrar considers
  necessary to establish that a vessel is required or entitled to be
  registered or is entitled to be listed or recorded.

### [Names of Vessels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51365 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Before registration or listing

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  vessel, other than one to be registered in the small vessel register,
  must be named in the form and manner specified by the Chief Registrar
  before it is registered or listed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of names

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar may, on application, approve the
  name of a vessel before it is registered or listed and approve a
  change in the name of a Canadian vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disallowance of names

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar must disallow a name if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is the same as the name of a Canadian vessel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is likely, in the opinion of the Chief
    Registrar, to be confused with the name of a Canadian vessel or with
    a distress signal;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is likely, in the opinion of the Chief
    Registrar, to be offensive to members of the public; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} its use is prohibited under an Act of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requiring renaming

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may order that a Canadian vessel be
  renamed if the Minister considers that its name would prejudice the
  international reputation of Canada.

### [Ownership of Vessels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51379 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of registration, the property in a vessel is divided into
  64 shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered owners

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4), only owners or
  joint owners of a vessel or of one or more shares in a vessel may be
  registered in the Register as owners of the vessel or shares, as the
  case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered owners --- financing
  agreements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a vessel described in paragraph 47(c)
  (a vessel subject to a financing agreement), the persons referred to
  in that paragraph are to be registered in the Register as the owners
  of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bare-boat charterers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a vessel described in section 48 (a
  bare-boat chartered vessel), no person may be registered in the
  Register as an owner of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of joint owners

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No more than five persons may be registered in the
  Register as joint owners of a vessel or a share in a vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of registered joint
  interests

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A registered jointly owned interest in a vessel or a
  share in a vessel may be disposed of only by the joint owners acting
  together.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of fractions prohibited

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} No person may be registered as the owner of a
  fractional part of a share in a vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No effect on beneficial owners

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} This section does not affect the beneficial interests
  of a person represented by or claiming through an owner of a vessel or
  a share in a vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trusts not recognized

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} No notice of a trust may be entered in the Register.

### [Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51399 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificates of registry

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  Chief Registrar is satisfied that all of the requirements of
  registration or listing have been met with respect to a vessel, the
  Chief Registrar must register or list the vessel, as the case may be,
  in the Register and issue a certificate of registry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every certificate of registry in respect of a vessel
  must contain the information specified by the Chief Registrar,
  including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its description;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its official number; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vessel described in paragraph
      47(b) (a vessel owned by a foreign corporation), the authorized
      representative,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vessel described in section 48
      (a bare-boat chartered vessel), the bare-boat charterer, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, its owner and the authorized
      representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every certificate of registry is valid for the period
  specified by the Chief Registrar.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provisional certificates

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Chief Registrar may, on application, issue a provisional certificate
  in respect of a vessel that is required or entitled to be registered
  under this Part if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vessel is in a foreign port and a person
    intends to register it under this Part; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vessel is in a port in Canada and the Chief
    Registrar is satisfied that permission to operate the vessel should
    be granted before a certificate of registry can be issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar may, on application, issue a
  provisional certificate in respect of a vessel that is not required or
  entitled to be registered under this Part if the Chief Registrar is
  satisfied that the vessel needs to undergo sea trials.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A provisional certificate is valid for the purpose
  and the period specified by the Chief Registrar.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An application for a provisional certificate must be
  made in the form and manner, include the information and be
  accompanied by the documents specified by the Chief Registrar.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lost certificates

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** If a certificate of
registry or provisional certificate is mislaid, lost or destroyed, the
Chief Registrar must issue a replacement certificate of registry or
provisional certificate, as the case may be, on application made by the
authorized representative or owner in the form and manner and including
the information and accompanied by the documents specified by the Chief
Registrar.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 56
- [2023, c. 26, s. 366](#1410310-1413754){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410310-1413754.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-56-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 56"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue, renew or amend
certificate

**[[56.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56.1 .sectionLabel}** Despite any other
provision of this Act, the Chief Registrar may refuse to issue, in
respect of a vessel, a certificate of registry, a provisional
certificate or a replacement certificate of registry or provisional
certificate, or to renew a certificate of registry or to amend one under
paragraph 73(b), if the applicant for, or holder of, the certificate is
in default of payment of a required fee, charge, cost or expense under
this Act or the [Wrecked, Abandoned or Hazardous Vessels
Act](/eng/acts/W-12.3) in respect of that vessel.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 366](#1410310-1413749){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410310-1413749.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Marking]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51426 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marking

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  authorized representative of a Canadian vessel shall, in the form and
  manner specified by the Chief Registrar, mark the vessel with its
  official number and any other information that the Chief Registrar
  specifies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of certificate of registry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A vessel's certificate of registry is not valid until
  the vessel has been marked in accordance with subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintenance of markings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative shall ensure that the
  vessel is kept marked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defacing, etc., markings

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall wilfully deface, alter, conceal or
  remove the markings of a Canadian vessel.

### [Notifying Chief Registrar]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51436 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of changes

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  authorized representative of a Canadian vessel shall notify the Chief
  Registrar within 30 days after any of the following occurs:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vessel is lost, wrecked or removed from
    service;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there has been a change in the owner's, the
    authorized representative's or a registered mortgagee's name or
    address;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the information provided with the application under
    section 51 has changed; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vessel described in section 48 (a
    bare-boat chartered vessel),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the right to fly the flag of the foreign state is
      reinstated, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the charterer ceases to have complete control
      and possession of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of alterations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a Canadian vessel is altered to the extent that it
  no longer corresponds with its description or particulars set out on
  the certificate of registry, the authorized representative shall,
  within 30 days after the alteration, notify the Chief Registrar and
  provide the Chief Registrar with the relevant information and
  documents.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of changes

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If for any reason a Canadian vessel does not have an
  authorized representative, its owner shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notify the Chief Registrar of that fact as soon as
    possible in the circumstances; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Chief Registrar within 30 days after any
    event referred to in subsection (1) or (2) occurs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of completion of
  construction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Within 30 days after completion of the construction
  of a vessel that is recorded as being built in Canada, the person in
  whose name the vessel is recorded shall notify the Chief Registrar of
  that fact and of the name and address of its owner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 58
- 2011, c. 15, s. 41(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 58"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Maintenance of Register]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51456 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** The Chief Registrar may
amend the Register or a certificate of registry to give effect to
changes of which the Chief Registrar has been notified under section 58
or to correct any clerical errors or obvious mistakes.

### [Suspension, Cancellation and Reinstatement of Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51459 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension and cancellation

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the
  Chief Registrar may suspend or cancel the registration or listing of a
  Canadian vessel if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is not marked in accordance with subsection
    57(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its certificate of registry has expired;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it does not have an authorized representative; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 58 has not been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the Chief Registrar must
  cancel the registration or listing of a Canadian vessel if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it has been lost, wrecked or removed from service;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is no longer required or entitled to be
    registered or entitled to be listed under this Part; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a registered vessel, a tonnage
    certificate provided by a tonnage measurer indicates that the vessel
    should be re-registered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice before cancellation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a Canadian vessel is not required or entitled to
  be registered under this Part after its ownership changes, the Chief
  Registrar must, before cancelling its registration under paragraph
  (2)(b), give the owners and registered mortgagees

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notice of the change in ownership; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an opportunity that, in the opinion of the Chief
    Registrar, is sufficient to transfer the vessel or shares in the
    vessel to a qualified person or to make an application under section
    74.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of registration

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of a vessel described in paragraph
  47(c) (a vessel subject to a financing agreement), the Chief Registrar
  must cancel the registration of a vessel if a person who acquires the
  vessel or a share in it does not, within the prescribed period,
  provide evidence that satisfies the Chief Registrar that the vessel is
  required or entitled to be registered under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of mortgages not affected

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** The cancellation of the
registration of a vessel does not affect the registration of mortgages
in respect of the vessel.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** The Chief Registrar may
reinstate the registration or listing of a vessel if, in the Chief
Registrar's opinion, the registration or listing of the vessel should
not have been cancelled.

### [Custody of Certificates of Registry and Provisional Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51482 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Carrying on board

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no person
  shall operate a vessel in respect of which a certificate of registry
  or provisional certificate has been issued unless the certificate is
  on board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who is in possession of a vessel's
  certificate of registry or provisional certificate shall deliver it to
  the person who is entitled to operate the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who is in possession of a certificate of
  registry or a provisional certificate issued under this Part shall
  deliver it to the Chief Registrar on request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention of certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A certificate of registry or provisional certificate
  is not subject to detention because of any title to, lien on, charge
  on or interest in the vessel that is claimed by an owner, a mortgagee,
  a charterer or an operator of the vessel, or by any other person.

### [Rights and Obligations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51492 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to fly Canadian flag

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  Canadian vessel has the right to fly the Canadian flag.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to fly flag

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The master of a Canadian vessel, other than one
  registered in the small vessel register, shall ensure that it flies
  the Canadian flag

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when signalled to do so by a government vessel or a
    vessel under the command of the Canadian Forces; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when entering or leaving, or while moored at or
    anchored in, a port.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar may, on application, suspend the
  registration of a Canadian vessel in respect of the right to fly the
  Canadian flag while the vessel is shown on the registry of a foreign
  state as a bare-boat chartered vessel.

### [Mortgages]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51502 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mortgage of vessel or share

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  owner of a vessel registered under this Part other than in the small
  vessel register, of a share in such a vessel or of a vessel recorded
  as being built in Canada may give the vessel or share, as the case may
  be, as security for a mortgage to be registered under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of mortgage

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A mortgage is to be filed with the Chief Registrar in
  the form and manner specified by the Chief Registrar.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date and time of registration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A mortgage is to be registered in the order in which
  it is filed, indicating the date and time of registration.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry of discharge of mortgage

**[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** On receipt of
satisfactory evidence that a mortgage has been discharged, the Chief
Registrar is to enter the discharge in the Register.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Priority of mortgages

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  more than one mortgage is registered in respect of the same vessel or
  share in a vessel, a mortgage registered before another mortgage has
  priority over that other mortgage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent to change in priority

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The priority of mortgages may be changed if all of
  the mortgagees file their written consent with the Chief Registrar.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mortgagee not treated as owner

**[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** A mortgage of a vessel or
a share in a vessel does not have the effect of the mortgagee becoming,
or the mortgagor ceasing to be, the owner of the vessel, except to the
extent necessary to make the vessel or share available as security under
the mortgage.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mortgagee has power of sale

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  mortgagee of a vessel or a share in a vessel has the absolute power,
  subject to any limitation set out in the registered mortgage, to sell
  the vessel or the share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If there is more than one registered mortgage of the
  same vessel or share, a subsequent mortgagee may not, except under an
  order of the Federal Court or of a court of competent jurisdiction
  whose rules provide for [in rem]{.otherLang lang="la"} procedure in
  respect of vessels, sell the vessel or share without the agreement of
  every prior mortgagee.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mortgage not affected by bankruptcy

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** The mortgage of a vessel
or a share in a vessel is not affected by the bankruptcy of the
mortgagor after the date of the registration of the mortgage, and the
mortgage is to be preferred to any right, claim or interest in the
vessel or share of the other creditors of the bankrupt or any trustee or
assignee on their behalf.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer of mortgages

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered mortgage of a vessel or a share in a vessel may be
  transferred to any person, in which case the instrument effecting the
  transfer must be filed in the form and manner specified by the Chief
  Registrar.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry of particulars

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar is to enter the particulars of
  the transfer in the Register.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmission of interest of mortgagee

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  interest of a mortgagee in a vessel or a share in a vessel is
  transmitted on death or bankruptcy, or by any lawful means other than
  by a transfer under section 71, the person to whom the interest is
  transmitted must file with the Chief Registrar the evidence of the
  transmission that the Chief Registrar specifies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry of particulars

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar is to enter the particulars of
  the transmission in the Register.

### [Transfers of Vessels or Shares in Vessels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51536 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** If the ownership of a
Canadian vessel or a share in one changes and the vessel is still
required or entitled to be registered under this Part,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the owner must provide the Chief Registrar with the
  evidence, including declarations, that the Chief Registrar considers
  necessary to establish that the vessel is required or entitled to be
  so registered; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Chief Registrar must amend the Register and the
  vessel's certificate of registry to reflect the change.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order for sale on acquisition by an
unqualified person

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** If an unqualified person
acquires a Canadian vessel, other than a vessel described in paragraph
47(b) (a vessel owned by a foreign corporation), a vessel described in
paragraph 47(c) (a vessel subject to a financing agreement) or a vessel
described in section 48 (a bare-boat chartered vessel), or a share in
one, any interested person may apply to the Federal Court, or any court
of competent jurisdiction whose rules provide for [in rem]{.otherLang
lang="la"} procedure in respect of vessels, for an order that the vessel
or share, as the case may be, be sold to a qualified person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of court to prohibit transfer

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** On the application of an
interested person, the Federal Court, or any court of competent
jurisdiction whose rules provide for [in rem]{.otherLang lang="la"}
procedure in respect of vessels, may make an order prohibiting any
dealing with a Canadian vessel or a share in one for a specified period.

### [Fleets]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51545 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for fleet

- **[[75.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An applicant may, instead of
  applying to have vessels individually registered in the small vessel
  register, apply to register a group of two or more vessels as a fleet
  in that register.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and manner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must be made in the form and manner,
  include the information and be accompanied by the documents specified
  by the Chief Registrar.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further evidence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the specified information and
  documents, the Chief Registrar may require an applicant to provide
  evidence, including declarations, that the Chief Registrar considers
  necessary to establish that the group of vessels may be registered as
  a fleet.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration --- fleet

- **[[75.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar may register a
  group of two or more vessels as a fleet if he or she is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all of the vessels are owned by the same owner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each vessel meets the requirements for registration
    in the small vessel register; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each vessel meets any other requirement ---
    including with respect to a vessel's dimensions, usage or propulsion
    --- that the Chief Registrar may establish for the vessels of a
    fleet.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small vessel register

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A fleet that is accepted for registration must be
  registered in the small vessel register.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of registry

- **[[75.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar must issue a
  certificate of registry in respect of a fleet that he or she
  registers, and the certificate is valid for the period that he or she
  specifies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A certificate of registry in respect of a fleet must
  contain the information specified by the Chief Registrar, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the fleet;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fleet's official number; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the owner and the
    authorized representative of the fleet.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Description --- number of vessels

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the fleet's description, the Chief Registrar must
  specify either the number of vessels that are to be part of the fleet
  or the minimum and maximum numbers of vessels that can be part of it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Official number

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The fleet's official number is also the official
  number of each vessel of that fleet.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized representative of fleet

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative of a fleet is the
  authorized representative, as determined under section 14, of the
  vessels of that fleet and must be the same authorized representative
  for all of the fleet's vessels.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2023, c. 26, s. 367\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acts or omissions of authorized
  representative binding

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The owner of a fleet is bound by the acts or
  omissions of the authorized representative of the fleet with respect
  to all matters assigned by this Act to that representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
- [2023, c. 26, s. 367](#1410312-1413758){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410312-1413758.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-75.03-20110626.html "Link to previous version of section 75.03"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue, renew or amend
certificate

**[[75.031]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.031 .sectionLabel}** Despite any other
provision of this Act, the Chief Registrar may refuse to issue or renew
a certificate of registry in respect of a fleet, or to amend one under
paragraph 75.14(b), if the applicant for, or holder of, the certificate
is in default of payment of a required fee, charge, cost or expense in
respect of that fleet or a vessel of that fleet under this Act or the
[Wrecked, Abandoned or Hazardous Vessels Act](/eng/acts/W-12.3).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 368](#1410314-1413762){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410314-1413762.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Addition or removal of vessels

**[[75.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.04 .sectionLabel}** Subject to
subsection 75.1(2), an owner of a fleet may, after the fleet is
registered, add a vessel to the fleet or remove a vessel from it.
However, any vessel that is to be added must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be owned by the same owner as all of the other
  vessels of the fleet;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} satisfy the conditions set out in paragraphs
  75.02(1)(b) and (c); and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fit within the description or particulars set out in
  the fleet's certificate of registry.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vessels registered

- **[[75.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a vessel
  that is or becomes part of a fleet is considered to be registered
  under this Part and, for greater certainty, is a Canadian vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No longer registered

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless it becomes part of another fleet, such a
  vessel ceases to be registered under this Part if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there is a change in its ownership; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is altered to the extent that it no longer fits
    within the description or particulars set out in the fleet's
    certificate of registry.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of individual registration

**[[75.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.06 .sectionLabel}** The Chief Registrar
may cancel the registration of a Canadian vessel if the vessel becomes
part of a fleet.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of provisions

**[[75.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.07 .sectionLabel}** The following
provisions do not apply in respect of a fleet or a vessel of a fleet:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsections 57(2) and (3);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 58;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} section 60;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 62;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsections 63(1) and (2);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} section 73.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

- **[[75.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.08
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the following
  provisions apply in respect of a fleet or a vessel of a fleet:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} section 56;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsections 57(1) and (4);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsections 63(3) and (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Section 59

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 59 applies in respect of a fleet, except that
  the reference to "section 58" is to be read as a reference to "section
  75.1".

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marking --- validity of fleet's
certificate of registry

- **[[75.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.09
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A fleet's certificate of registry is
  not valid unless each of the fleet's vessels has been marked in
  accordance with subsection 57(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintenance of markings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative of a fleet shall ensure
  that each of the fleet's vessels is kept marked.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of changes --- name and
address

- **[[75.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative of a
  fleet shall notify the Chief Registrar within 30 days after there has
  been a change in the owner's or authorized representative's name or
  address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of changes --- number of
  vessels

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the number of vessels in a fleet changes to the
  extent that the fleet no longer corresponds with its description set
  out on the certificate of registry, its authorized representative
  shall, within 30 days after the change in number, notify the Chief
  Registrar and provide him or her with the relevant information and
  documents.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of changes --- owner

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If for any reason a fleet does not have an authorized
  representative, its owner shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notify the Chief Registrar of that fact as soon as
    possible in the circumstances; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Chief Registrar within 30 days after any
    event referred to in subsection (1) or (2) occurs.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension and cancellation

- **[[75.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the
  Chief Registrar may suspend or cancel the registration of a fleet if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any one of the fleet's vessels is not marked in
    accordance with subsection 57(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fleet's certificate of registry has expired;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the fleet does not have an authorized
    representative; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 75.1 has not been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation --- fleet

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the Chief Registrar must
  cancel the registration of a fleet if it no longer qualifies for
  registration under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Registrar must cancel the registration of a
  fleet if a person who acquires the fleet does not, within the
  prescribed period, provide evidence that satisfies the Chief Registrar
  that the fleet still qualifies for registration under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement

**[[75.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.12 .sectionLabel}** The Chief Registrar
may reinstate the registration of a fleet if, in his or her opinion, the
registration of the fleet should not have been cancelled.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of certificate

**[[75.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.13 .sectionLabel}** A person who is in
possession of a fleet's certificate of registry shall deliver it to the
person who is entitled to operate the fleet.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of ownership

**[[75.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.14 .sectionLabel}** If the ownership of
a fleet changes and the fleet still qualifies to be registered under
this Part,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the owner must provide the Chief Registrar with the
  evidence, including declarations, that the Chief Registrar considers
  necessary to establish that the fleet still qualifies to be so
  registered; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Chief Registrar must amend the Register and the
  certificate of registry to reflect the change.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

### [Entries]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51670 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of entries

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** A person may examine or
obtain copies of any entries in the Register with respect to a vessel or
fleet.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 76
- 2011, c. 15, s. 42
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 76"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51675 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make regulations for
carrying out the purposes and provisions of this Part, including
regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the registration of vessels and fleets and
  the listing and recording of vessels;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the issuance and renewal of certificates
  of registry;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the suspension and cancellation of the
  registration of a Canadian vessel or a fleet, and the suspension and
  cancellation of the listing of a Canadian vessel;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the naming and marking of vessels;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting the port of registration;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the form and manner of notifying the Chief
  Registrar under sections 58 and 75.1;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} respecting the evidence that owners of vessels
  previously registered in a foreign state must provide to prove that
  the vessels are no longer registered in the foreign state;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting the calculation of the tonnage of vessels
  and the issuance of certificates of tonnage; and

- [(h.1)]{.lawlabel} respecting the exemption of vessels or classes of
  vessels from the registration requirement in subsection 46(1);

- [(h.2)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the Minister to exempt, by order,
  vessels or classes of vessels from the registration requirement in
  subsection 46(1) for the period specified in the regulations and on
  any terms and conditions that he or she considers appropriate, if he
  or she is of the opinion that the exemption is not likely to adversely
  affect marine safety, and authorizing the Minister to amend or revoke
  an exemption;

- [(h.3)]{.lawlabel} respecting an authorization under paragraph (h.2);
  and

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that may be prescribed under
  this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 77
- 2011, c. 15, s. 43
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-77-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 77"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51692 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person commits an offence who contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 57(4) (wilfully defacing, altering,
    concealing or removing markings); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under paragraph
    77(h).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than one
  year, or to both.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person commits an offence who contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 46(2) (register vessel);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order made under subsection 52(4) (renaming of
    vessel);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 57(1) (mark vessel);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subsection 57(3) (maintenance of markings);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 58(1) (notify of changes --- authorized
    representative);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 58(2) (notify of alteration ---
    authorized representative);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} subsection 58(3) (notify if no authorized
    representative --- owner);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} subsection 58(4) (notify of completion of
    construction);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 63(1) (operation of vessel without a
    certificate on board);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} subsection 63(2) (deliver certificate to person
    entitled to operate vessel);

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} subsection 63(3) (deliver certificate to Chief
    Registrar);

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} subsection 64(2) (fly Canadian flag);

  - [(l.1)]{.lawlabel} subsection 75.09(2) (maintenance of markings);

  - [(l.2)]{.lawlabel} subsection 75.1(1) (notification of changes ---
    name and address);

  - [(l.3)]{.lawlabel} subsection 75.1(2) (notification of changes ---
    number of vessels);

  - [(l.4)]{.lawlabel} subsection 75.1(3) (notification of changes ---
    owner);

  - [(l.5)]{.lawlabel} section 75.13 (delivery of certificate); or

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under any of
    paragraphs 77(a) to (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$25,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing offence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an offence under paragraph (1)(a) or (c) is
  committed or continued on more than one day, the person who committed
  it is liable to be convicted for a separate offence for each day on
  which it is committed or continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 79
- 2011, c. 15, s. 44
- [2023, c. 26, s. 369](#1410316-1413765){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410316-1413765.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-79-20110626.html "Link to previous version of section 79"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Personnel]{.HTitleText1} {#h-51727 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51728 .Subheading}

Definition of [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Transport.

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51731 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canadian vessels

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of Canadian vessels, other than pleasure craft, everywhere.
Subsections 86(2) to (4) also apply in respect of foreign vessels in
Canadian waters.

### [Masters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51734 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of documents

- **[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  master of a Canadian vessel shall ensure that every person who is
  employed in a position on board presents to the master all Canadian
  maritime documents that they are required under this Part to have for
  that position.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sufficient and competent staff

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No master of a Canadian vessel shall operate it
  unless it is staffed with a crew that is sufficient and competent for
  the safe operation of the vessel on its intended voyage, and is kept
  so staffed during the voyage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obstruction prohibited

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No crew member shall wilfully obstruct a master's
  operation of a Canadian vessel unless the master is, without just
  cause, putting at risk the safety of the vessel or of any person on
  board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention of persons

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  master of a Canadian vessel may detain any person on board if the
  master has reasonable grounds to believe that it is necessary to do so
  to maintain good order and discipline on the vessel or for the safety
  of the vessel or of persons or property on board. The detention may
  last only as long as necessary to maintain order and discipline or to
  ensure the safety of persons or property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Custody

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The master of a Canadian vessel on a voyage may take
  into custody without warrant any person on board who the master has
  reasonable grounds to believe has committed an offence under this Act
  or any other Act of Parliament, and must as soon as feasible deliver
  that person to a peace officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of force on a voyage

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The master of a Canadian vessel on a voyage is
  justified in using as much force as the master believes on reasonable
  grounds is necessary for the purpose of maintaining good order and
  discipline on the vessel, but the master must not use force that is
  intended or is likely to cause death or grievous bodily harm unless
  the master believes on reasonable grounds that it is necessary for
  self-preservation or the preservation of anyone on the vessel from
  death or grievous bodily harm.

### [Stowaways and Other Persons]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51749 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liable for discipline

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** Every person whom the
master of a Canadian vessel is compelled to take on board and convey,
and every person who stows away on a Canadian vessel or hides in cargo
that is subsequently loaded on a Canadian vessel, is, as long as the
person remains on board, subject to the same rules and orders for
preserving discipline, and to the same punishments for contravening the
rules or orders constituting or tending to a breach of discipline, as
are crew members.

### [Contract of Employment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51752 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Masters' contracts

- **[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  every contract of employment between the authorized representative and
  the master of a Canadian vessel there is implied, notwithstanding any
  agreement to the contrary, an obligation on the authorized
  representative that the authorized representative and every agent
  charged with loading the vessel, preparing it for a voyage or sending
  it on a voyage use all reasonable means to ensure its seaworthiness
  for the voyage when the voyage commences and to keep the vessel in a
  seaworthy condition during the voyage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Crew members' contracts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In every contract of employment between the
  authorized representative and a crew member of a Canadian vessel there
  is implied, notwithstanding any agreement to the contrary, an
  obligation on the authorized representative that the authorized
  representative, the master and every agent charged with loading the
  vessel, preparing it for a voyage or sending it on a voyage use all
  reasonable means to ensure its seaworthiness for the voyage when the
  voyage commences and to keep the vessel in a seaworthy condition
  during the voyage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this section subjects the authorized
  representative of a Canadian vessel to any liability by reason of the
  vessel's being sent to sea in an unseaworthy condition if sending the
  vessel to sea in that condition was reasonable and justifiable in
  order to mitigate unsafe circumstances.

### [Liens and Claims]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51760 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liens

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  master, and each crew member, of a Canadian vessel has a maritime lien
  against the vessel for claims that arise in respect of their
  employment on the vessel, including in respect of wages and costs of
  repatriation that are payable to the master or crew member under any
  law or custom.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Foreign liens

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The master and each crew member of a vessel on whom a
  maritime lien against the vessel is conferred by a jurisdiction other
  than Canada in respect of employment on the vessel has a maritime lien
  against the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liens for necessaries

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The master of a Canadian vessel has a maritime lien
  against the vessel for claims that arise in respect of disbursements
  made or liabilities incurred by the master for necessaries on account
  of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claims

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The master, and each crew member, of a vessel may
  maintain an action against the vessel in the Federal Court, or any
  court of competent jurisdiction whose rules provide for [in
  rem]{.otherLang lang="la"} procedure in respect of vessels, for claims
  in respect of which a lien is conferred by subsection (1), (2) or
  (2.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Priority

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Liens conferred by subsection (1) or (2) rank in
  priority to all other claims, secured or unsecured, against the
  vessel, other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} claims for costs relating to the arrest and the
    judicial sale of the vessel; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any lien arising from a claim for salvage in
    respect of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Priority --- liens for necessaries

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Liens conferred by subsection (2.1) rank in priority
  to all other claims, secured or unsecured, against the vessel, other
  than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} liens conferred by subsection (1) or (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} claims for costs relating to the arrest and the
    judicial sale of the vessel; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any lien arising from a claim for salvage in
    respect of the vessel.

### [Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51779 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Positions on board Canadian vessels

**[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is
employed on board a Canadian vessel in a position in respect of which a
certificate is required under this Part shall hold the certificate and
comply with its terms and conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 87
- 2017, c. 26, s. 41(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-87-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 87"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility

- **[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Only a
  Canadian citizen or a permanent resident within the meaning of
  subsection 2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) may hold a certificate of competency that is
  issued under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Foreign certificates of competency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, on application by a person
  described in subsection (1), issue a certificate of competency in
  respect of certain requirements under this Act to the holder of a
  certificate of competency that was issued under the laws of a foreign
  state if the Minister is satisfied that the requirements under those
  laws for the foreign certificate meet or exceed the requirements under
  this Act. Before issuing the certificate, the Minister may require
  that the holder take an examination set by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, ss. 88, 323
- 2017, c. 26, s. 41(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-88-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 88"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acceptance of foreign certificates

- **[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  government of a foreign state has entered into a reciprocal
  arrangement with the Government of Canada to accept certificates of
  competency issued under this Part in lieu of certificates of
  competency of that state and if the Minister is satisfied that the
  requirements under the laws of the foreign state for a certificate of
  competency meet or exceed the requirements under this Act, the
  Minister may direct, subject to any conditions that the Minister
  specifies, that the foreign certificate may be accepted in lieu of a
  certificate of competency issued under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign certificate that is accepted under
  subsection (1) may, in so far only as concerns its validity in Canada,
  be suspended or cancelled by the Minister as though it were a Canadian
  maritime document, and the holder of any certificate so suspended or
  cancelled must deliver it to the Minister, who must then return it to
  the authority that issued it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 89
- 2017, c. 26, s. 41(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-89-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 89"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Medical or Optometric Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51798 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister to be provided with information

- **[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  physician or an optometrist believes on reasonable grounds that the
  holder of a certificate issued under this Part has a medical or
  optometric condition that is likely to constitute a hazard to maritime
  safety, the physician or optometrist shall inform the Minister without
  delay of that opinion and the reasons for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patient to advise

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a certificate issued under this Part in
  respect of which standards of medical or optometric fitness are
  required shall, before being examined by a physician or an
  optometrist, advise the physician or optometrist that they hold the
  certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use by Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may use any information provided under
  subsection (1) for the purpose of determining whether the holder of a
  certificate meets the requirements in respect of that certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No proceedings shall lie

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No legal, disciplinary or other proceedings lie
  against a physician or optometrist for anything they do in good faith
  in compliance with this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed consent

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a certificate is deemed, for the
  purposes of this section, to have consented to the Minister being
  informed under subsection (1) in the circumstances referred to in that
  subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 90
- 2017, c. 26, s. 42(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-90-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 90"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Articles of Agreement, Discharge and Record of Sea Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51812 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Articles of agreement

- **[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  master of a vessel who is required by the regulations made under this
  Part to enter into articles of agreement shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that every crew member has entered into and
    received articles of agreement, in the form and manner specified by
    the Minister, with respect to their position on the vessel; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} display, in a location that is accessible to the
    crew, the provisions of the articles of agreement that are common to
    each crew member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The articles of agreement between the master and a
  crew member must state the surname and other names of the crew member,
  the respective rights and obligations of each of the parties and any
  other information required by the regulations made under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discharge

**[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** When a crew member of a
Canadian vessel is discharged, the authorized representative shall
provide the member with a certificate of discharge in the form and
manner specified by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of sea service

- **[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  authorized representative and every crew member of a Canadian vessel
  shall each maintain, in the form and manner and for the period
  specified by the Minister, a record of sea service of the member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies to the Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On request, the authorized representative shall
  provide the Minister with a copy of, or an extract from, a crew
  member's record of sea service.

### [Return of Crew Members]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51827 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return and payment of expenses

- **[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations and
  except in the case of desertion or mutual agreement, if a crew member
  is left behind when a Canadian vessel sails or is shipwrecked, the
  authorized representative shall ensure that arrangements are made to
  return the crew member to the place where they first came on board or
  to another place to which they have agreed, and pay the expenses of
  returning the crew member as well as all expenses, including medical
  expenses, that the crew member reasonably incurs before being
  returned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception if insurance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative is not responsible to
  pay any expenses covered by insurance for which the authorized
  representative paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If authorized representative does not
  comply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the authorized representative does not comply with
  subsection (1), the Minister may act in place of the authorized
  representative and any expenses incurred by the Minister constitute a
  debt due to Her Majesty in right of Canada by the authorized
  representative and may be recovered as such in a court of competent
  jurisdiction.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Desertion or serious violation of
contract

**[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** If a crew member deserts
a Canadian vessel or has committed a serious violation of their contract
of employment, the authorized representative or, if the authorized
representative entered into an agreement with another person to provide
the crew member, that person may return the crew member to the place
where they first came on board or to another place on which they and the
authorized representative or the other person, as the case may be, have
agreed. The expenses of returning the crew member may be deducted from
any remuneration due to them.

### [Births and Deaths]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51837 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Informing the Minister

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** The authorized
representative of a Canadian vessel must, in the form and manner
specified by the Minister, inform the Minister of every birth or death
on board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Death of crew member

- **[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any other law, if a crew
  member of a Canadian vessel dies, the master shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} without delay inform the Minister or a diplomatic
    or consular officer of Canada of the circumstances surrounding the
    death; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the option of the person, if any, whom the crew
    member identified as a contact in case of emergency, arrange to bury
    or cremate the body or, if the person and the master agree on a
    place to return the body to, return it to that place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cremation or burial

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the contact person referred to in paragraph (1)(b)
  cannot be consulted within a reasonable period, the master shall,
  subject to any other law, bury or cremate the body, taking into
  account the deceased's wishes if known.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptional circumstances

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the master, it is not feasible,
  because of the type of voyage or the lack of facilities, to follow the
  wishes of the contact person referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or of the
  deceased crew member, the master shall, subject to any other law, bury
  or cremate the body.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Property of a deceased crew member

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative of a Canadian vessel
  shall give any property that belonged to a deceased crew member and
  was on board to the contact person referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or
  the deceased crew member's estate or succession.

### [Obligation of Persons Who Provide Crew Members]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51851 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If an agreement to provide crew members

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** If the authorized
representative of a Canadian vessel entered into an agreement with
another person to provide crew members, that other person shall, in lieu
of the authorized representative or the master with respect to those
crew members, comply with the obligation of the authorized
representative or master set out in

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 91(1)(a) (enter into articles);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 92 (provide certificate of discharge);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 93(1) (maintain record of sea service);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} subsection 93(2) (provide copy of record of sea
  service); and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 94(1) (pay expenses), except in respect of
  any expenses covered by insurance for which that other person or the
  authorized representative paid.

### [Resolution of Disputes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51859 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjudication by Minister

**[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, on the
request of the authorized representative or a crew member of a Canadian
vessel, adjudicate any dispute between the authorized representative and
crew member that arises under this Part. The Minister's decision is
binding on the parties.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 99
- [2023, c. 26, s. 370(E)](#1410318-1413770){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410318-1413770.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-99-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 99"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51862 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make regulations for
carrying out the purposes and provisions of this Part, including
regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifying the positions that shall be occupied on
  board Canadian vessels, or classes of Canadian vessels, their minimum
  number and the types and classes of Canadian maritime documents that
  persons in those positions shall hold;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifying the requirements in respect of any
  position on board Canadian vessels or classes of Canadian vessels;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} specifying the types and classes of certificates that
  may be issued in respect of positions on board Canadian vessels or
  classes of Canadian vessels;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the qualifications required of applicants
  for any type or class of certificate specified under paragraph (c),
  including their medical fitness, minimum age, degree of knowledge,
  skill, training and experience;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner of determining whether a person
  meets the requirements specified under paragraph (b) in respect of a
  position or the qualifications required under paragraph (d) for any
  type or class of certificate of competency or other Canadian maritime
  document;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} specifying the terms and conditions of certificates
  of competency or other Canadian maritime documents issued under this
  Part;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} specifying the circumstances in which ensuring
  arrangements for a crew member's return and paying their expenses are
  not required for the purpose of subsection 94(1);

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting persons who enter into agreements to
  provide crew members, including requiring that those persons be
  licensed;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} specifying the Canadian vessels or classes of
  Canadian vessels in respect of which the master shall enter into
  articles of agreement with crew members;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} specifying the information that must be contained in
  articles of agreement;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} specifying what constitutes a serious violation of a
  contract of employment;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} respecting any occupational health or safety matter
  on board a vessel that is not regulated by the [Canada Labour
  Code](/eng/acts/L-2); and

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} respecting the payment and allotment of crew members'
  wages.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 100
- 2017, c. 26, s. 43(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-100-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 100"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51880 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person commits an offence who
  contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 82(2) (operating a vessel without
    sufficient and competent crew);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 82(3) (wilfully obstructing the
    operation of a vessel); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under any of
    paragraphs 100(a) to (i) and (k) to (m).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than 18
  months, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing offence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an offence under subsection (1) is committed or
  continued on more than one day, the person who committed it is liable
  to be convicted for a separate offence for each day on which it is
  committed or continued.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act

- **[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person commits an offence who
  contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} section 87 (hold certificate or document and comply
    with its terms and conditions);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 90(1) (inform Minister of medical
    opinion);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 90(2) (inform that holder of a
    certificate);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subsection 94(1) (pay expenses);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 97(1), (2), (3) or (4) (take measures
    following a death); or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 98(e) (failure to pay expenses).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than one
  year, or to both.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person commits an offence who
  contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 82(1) (ensure employees present Canadian
    maritime documents);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 91(1)(a) (enter into articles);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 91(1)(b) (display articles);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 92 (provide certificate of discharge);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 93(1) (maintain record of sea service);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 93(2) (provide copy of record of sea
    service);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 98(a) (enter into articles);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 98(b) (provide certificate of discharge);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 98(c) (maintain record of sea service);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 98(d) (provide copy of record of sea
    service); or

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under paragraph
    100(j).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$25,000.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 103
- [2023, c. 26, s. 371](#1410320-1413775){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410320-1413775.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-103-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 103"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Safety]{.HTitleText1} {#h-51918 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51919 .Subheading}

Definition of [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Transport.

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51922 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canadian vessels and foreign vessels

**[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of Canadian vessels, other than pleasure craft, everywhere and
in respect of foreign vessels in Canadian waters.

### [Authorized Representatives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51925 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General duties

- **[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  authorized representative of a Canadian vessel shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the vessel and its machinery and
    equipment meet the requirements of the regulations made under this
    Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} develop procedures for the safe operation of the
    vessel and for dealing with emergencies; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the crew and passengers receive safety
    training.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties re Canadian maritime documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative of a Canadian vessel
  shall ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vessel and its machinery and equipment are
