    inspected for the purpose of obtaining all of the Canadian maritime
    documents that are required under this Part; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every term or condition attached to a Canadian
    maritime document issued in respect of the vessel or its machinery
    or equipment is met.

### [Masters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51936 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obtaining Canadian maritime documents

**[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** The master of a
Canadian vessel shall, before the vessel embarks on a voyage from a port
in Canada, ensure that all of the Canadian maritime documents required
under this Part have been obtained.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions

- **[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the master of a Canadian vessel has had the vessel or its machinery or
  equipment inspected by an authority of a foreign state and that
  authority has issued a certificate that certifies that the vessel,
  machinery or equipment meets certain requirements of that state and
  the Minister is satisfied that those requirements meet or exceed the
  requirements for a Canadian maritime document required under this
  Part, the Minister may grant an exemption, for the period specified by
  the Minister, from the obligation to comply with subsection 106(2) and
  section 107 with respect to that certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of certificates by foreign
  states

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may request the government of a state
  that is a party to a convention, protocol or resolution listed in
  Schedule 1 respecting the safety of vessels or of persons who are on
  board or are loading or unloading vessels to issue in respect of a
  Canadian vessel any certificate provided for by the convention,
  protocol or resolution. A certificate issued as a result of such a
  request and containing a statement that it has been so issued has
  effect for the purposes of this Act as if it had been issued under
  this Part and may be suspended or cancelled as though it were a
  Canadian maritime document.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety of persons

- **[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  master of a vessel shall take all reasonable steps to ensure the
  safety of the vessel and of persons who are on board or are loading or
  unloading it while using equipment on it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection from hazards

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the master of a vessel is informed of a safety
  hazard, the master shall, unless the master determines that the hazard
  does not exist, take reasonable measures to protect the vessel and
  persons on board from the hazard, including eliminating it if
  feasible. If it is not feasible to eliminate it, the master of a
  Canadian vessel shall notify the authorized representative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Carrying excess number of persons

- **[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  master of a vessel must ensure that the number of persons carried on
  board is not more than the number of persons authorized to be on board
  under any certificate issued under this Part or under an international
  convention, protocol or resolution listed in Schedule 1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submerging load lines

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The master of a vessel shall ensure that the
  applicable load lines on the vessel are not submerged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to subsection (1)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a person
  carried on board a vessel in pursuance of the obligation on the master
  to carry shipwrecked or distressed persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 110
- [2023, c. 26, s. 372](#1410322-1413779){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410322-1413779.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-110-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 110"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to cease --- master

- **[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may direct a master to cease any operation that, in the
  Minister's opinion, poses an undue risk because of unsafe conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to take measures --- master

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may direct a master to take measures
  that, in the Minister's opinion, are necessary to avoid an undue risk
  because of unsafe conditions, including a direction to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide the Minister with any information that the
    Minister considers appropriate to assess or deal with risks to
    marine safety;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} proceed by the route and in the manner that the
    Minister specifies; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} proceed to a place that the Minister selects, by
    the route and in the manner that the Minister specifies, and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} unload the vessel's cargo, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} moor, anchor or remain there for any reasonable
      period that the Minister specifies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to authorize vessel

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may direct a port authority or a person
  in charge of a port authority or place to authorize a vessel in
  respect of which a direction has been made under paragraph (2)(c) to
  proceed to the place selected by the Minister and to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unload the cargo; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} moor, anchor or remain there for any reasonable
    period that the Minister may specify.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 111
- [2023, c. 26, s. 373](#1410324-1413796){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410324-1413796.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-111-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 111"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to be sent respecting dangers
to navigation

**[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** If the master of a
Canadian vessel encounters dangerous ice, a dangerous derelict or other
direct danger to navigation, a tropical storm, winds of a force of 10 or
more on the Beaufort scale for which no storm warning has been received
or subfreezing air temperatures associated with gale force winds and
causing severe ice accretion on the superstructure of the vessel, the
master shall give notice to all vessels in the vicinity and the
prescribed authorities on shore of the danger.

### [Crew]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51960 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Carrying out duties and reporting

**[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** Every crew member on
board a vessel shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} carry out their duties and functions in a manner that
  does not jeopardize the safety of the vessel or of any person on
  board;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} report to the master any safety hazards of which they
  become aware;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} report to the master any change in their
  circumstances that could affect their ability to carry out their
  duties and functions safely; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} comply with lawful directions given by the master.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to cease --- crew

- **[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may direct a crew member on board a vessel to cease any
  operation that, in the Minister's opinion, poses an undue risk because
  of unsafe conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to take measures --- crew

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may direct a crew member on board a
  vessel to take measures that, in the Minister's opinion, are necessary
  to avoid an undue risk because of unsafe conditions, including a
  direction to provide the Minister with any information that the
  Minister considers appropriate to support assessing or addressing
  risks to marine safety.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 114
- [2023, c. 26, s. 374](#1410326-1413804){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410326-1413804.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-114-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 114"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Passengers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51969 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with directions

- **[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every passenger on board a vessel
  shall comply with any direction that is given to them by the master or
  a crew member to carry out the provisions of this Act or the
  regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with direction to leave a
  vessel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every passenger on board a vessel shall comply with a
  direction to leave the vessel that is given to them by the master
  before the vessel embarks on a voyage.

### [Authorized Representatives, Masters, Crew Members and Other Persons]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51975 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When boarding a vessel prohibited

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 135
(stopping and boarding a vessel), 175.1 (powers of pollution response
officers --- general), 196 and 198 (pleasure craft inspections), 200
(stopping and boarding a vessel) and 211 (inspections) and to any other
Act of Parliament, no person shall go or attempt to go on board a vessel
or to leave or attempt to leave one

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} without the permission or against the orders of the
  master or the person in charge of embarkation or disembarkation; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there is no safe means of embarkation or
  disembarkation or every such means has been blocked.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 116
- 2005, c. 29, s. 17
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-116-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 116"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tampering and vandalism

**[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** No person shall tamper
with or vandalize a vessel or its machinery, equipment or notices or
plans relating to emergency procedures, safety or navigation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jeopardizing safety

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** No person shall take
any action that might jeopardize the safety of a vessel or of persons on
board.

### [Construction of Vessels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51986 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}In accordance with plans

**[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, no person shall construct, manufacture or alter a vessel of
a prescribed class otherwise than in accordance with plans approved by
the Minister as having met the requirements of the regulations
respecting the design and construction of vessels of that class.

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-51989 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make
  regulations respecting the safety of vessels or classes of vessels and
  of persons on board or loading or unloading a vessel, including
  regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the design, construction, manufacture
    and maintenance of vessels or classes of vessels;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifying the machinery, equipment and supplies
    that are required or prohibited on board vessels or classes of
    vessels;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the design, construction, manufacture,
    maintenance, storage, testing, approval, arrangement and use of a
    vessel's or a class of vessels' machinery, equipment and supplies;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the requirements that vessels, or
    classes of vessels, and their machinery and equipment must meet;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} requiring the obtaining of certificates certifying
    that any of the requirements referred to in paragraph (d) are met;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} specifying the terms and conditions of certificates
    issued under this Part;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} respecting the inspection and testing of vessels,
    or classes of vessels, and their machinery, equipment and supplies;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting load lines and draught marks on vessels
    or classes of vessels;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} respecting procedures and practices that must be
    followed;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} respecting compulsory routes and recommended
    routes;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} regulating or prohibiting the operation of vessels
    for the purpose of protecting persons, vessels, artificial islands,
    installations, structures, works, shore areas or environmentally
    sensitive areas;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} respecting the prevention of collisions in Canadian
    waters or waters in the exclusive economic zone of Canada;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} respecting arrangements for ensuring communication
    between persons in different parts of a vessel and between persons
    on board and other persons;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} respecting information and documents that must be
    supplied to the master and kept on board vessels or classes of
    vessels;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} respecting the marking of vessels and the posting
    of notices, plans and Canadian maritime documents to show
    information relating to safety and to emergency procedures;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} respecting the number of passengers that may be on
    board a vessel and their safety;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} respecting the illumination of docks or wharfs at
    which vessels are berthed;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} respecting shore-based gangways;

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} respecting cargo;

  - [(s.1)]{.lawlabel} respecting arrangements for emergency services,
    including requiring vessels or classes of vessels to enter into such
    arrangements; and

  - [(t)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that may be prescribed under
    this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under this section apply in respect
  of vessels that are capable of engaging in the drilling for, or the
  production, conservation or processing of, oil or gas only if the
  regulations so state and were made on the joint recommendation of the
  Minister and the Minister of Natural Resources.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Aircraft

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 105, regulations referred to in
  paragraph (1)(k) or (l) may be made in respect of aircraft on or over
  Canadian waters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pleasure craft

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 105, regulations referred to in
  paragraph (1)(j), (k) or (l) may be made in respect of pleasure craft
  that are in Canadian waters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contraventions of regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 105, paragraph 121(1)(s) applies in
  respect of contraventions of regulations made under subsection (3) or
  (4).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 120
- 2018, c. 27, s. 694(E)
- [2023, c. 26, s. 375](#1410328-1413807){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410328-1413807.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-120-20181213.html "Link to previous version of section 120"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52023 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that,
  contravenes any of the following commits an offence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 106(1)(a) (ensure vessel meets
    requirements);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 106(1)(b) (develop emergency procedures);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 106(1)(c) (ensure training);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 106(2)(a) (ensure vessel inspected);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 106(2)(b) (ensure terms and conditions
    met);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} section 107 (obtain Canadian maritime documents);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} subsection 109(1) (ensure safety);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} subsection 109(2) (protect from hazards and notify
    authorized representative);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 110(1) (too many persons);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subsection 111(1)
    (direction to cease --- master);

  - [(j.1)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subsection 111(2)
    (direction to take measures --- master);

  - [(j.2)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subsection 111(3)
    (direction to authorize vessel);

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} section 112 (inform of danger);

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 113(a) (carry out duties and functions
    safely);

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 113(b) (report safety hazards);

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 113(c) (report change in circumstances);

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 113(d) (comply with lawful direction
    given by master);

  - [(o.1)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subsection 114(2)
    (direction to take measures --- crew);

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} section 117 (tampering or vandalism);

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} section 118 (jeopardizing safety);

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} section 119 (constructing, manufacturing or
    altering a vessel not in accordance with approved plans); and

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under this
    Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that, commits an offence
  under subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not
  more than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than
  18 months, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 121
- [2023, c. 26, s. 376](#1410330-1413815){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410330-1413815.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-121-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 121"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of subsection 110(2)

**[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
contravenes subsection 110(2) (submerged load lines) commits an offence
and is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more than \$500,000
in respect of each centimetre or part of a centimetre that the
applicable load line is submerged or to imprisonment for a term of not
more than 18 months, or to both.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act

- **[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person commits an offence who
  contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subsection 114(1)
    (direction to cease --- crew);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 115(1) (passenger to comply with
    direction);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 115(2) (passenger to comply with
    direction to leave vessel);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 116(a) (boarding or attempting to board
    without permission); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 116(b) (boarding or attempting to board
    after safety barriers are in place).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than one
  year, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 123
- [2023, c. 26, s. 377](#1410337-1413819){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410337-1413819.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-123-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 123"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When compliance agreement in effect

**[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124 .sectionLabel}** If an offence under any
of sections 121 to 123 is committed while an agreement or arrangement is
in effect between the Minister and the authorized representative of a
Canadian vessel that provides that inspections of the vessel to ensure
compliance with a provision referred to in subsection 121(1), section
122 or subsection 123(1) will be carried out by the authorized
representative or a person or an organization acting on their behalf,
the amount of any fine imposed under that section may be doubled.

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Navigation Services]{.HTitleText1} {#h-52062 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52063 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[aid to navigation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aid to navigation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a buoy, beacon,
    lighthouse, landmark, radio aid to marine navigation or any other
    structure or device installed, built or maintained in or on water or
    on land for the purpose of assisting with marine navigation. ([aide
    à la navigation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Fisheries and
    Oceans. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[VTS Zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[VTS Zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Vessel Traffic Services
    Zone established under paragraph 136(1)(a). ([zone
    STM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [Vessel Traffic Services]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52069 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entering, leaving or proceeding within a
VTS Zone

- **[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), no vessel
  of a prescribed class shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} enter, leave or proceed within a VTS Zone without
    having previously obtained a clearance under this section; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} proceed within a VTS Zone unless able to maintain
    direct communication with a marine communications and traffic
    services officer in accordance with the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marine communications and traffic
  services officers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may designate as marine communications
  and traffic services officers persons in the federal public
  administration who meet the requirements specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of officers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made under section 136, to
  any other Act of Parliament governing ports or harbours and to the
  regulations or by-laws made under such an Act, for the purpose of
  promoting safe and efficient navigation or environmental protection, a
  marine communications and traffic services officer may, with respect
  to any vessel of a prescribed class that is about to enter or is
  within a VTS Zone,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} grant a clearance to the vessel to enter, leave or
    proceed within the VTS Zone;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct the master, pilot or person in charge of the
    deck watch of the vessel to provide, in the manner and at any time
    that may be specified in the direction, any pertinent information in
    respect of the vessel that may be specified in the direction;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} direct the vessel to use any radio frequencies in
    communications with coast stations or any other vessel that may be
    specified in the direction; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} direct the vessel, at the time, between the times
    or before or after any event that may be specified in the direction,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to leave the VTS Zone,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to leave or refrain from entering any area
      within the VTS Zone that may be specified in the direction, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to proceed to or remain at any location within
      the VTS Zone that may be specified in the direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If vessel unable to communicate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a vessel

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is unable to obtain a clearance required by
    subsection (1) because of an inability to establish direct
    communication with a marine communications and traffic services
    officer, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after obtaining a clearance, is unable to maintain
    direct communication with a marine communications and traffic
    services officer,

  the vessel may, subject to subsection (6), nevertheless proceed on its
  route.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication and obtaining clearance

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The master shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the circumstances described in subsection (4),
    take all reasonable measures to communicate with a marine
    communications and traffic services officer as soon as possible; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the circumstances described in paragraph (4)(a),
    obtain a clearance as soon as possible after direct communication is
    established.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equipment failure

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the circumstances described in subsection (4), if
  the master is unable to establish or maintain direct communication
  because of an equipment failure on the vessel, the vessel shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if it is in a port or anchorage where the equipment
    can be repaired, remain there until it is able to establish
    communication in accordance with the regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if it is not in a port or anchorage where the
    equipment can be repaired, proceed to the nearest reasonably safe
    port or anchorage on its route when it is safe to do so and remain
    there until it is able to establish communication in accordance with
    the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 126
- 2003, c. 22, s. 224(E)
- [2023, c. 26, s. 423(F)](#1410504-1414019){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410504-1414019.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-126-20050401.html "Link to previous version of section 126"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variations from requirements or
conditions

- **[[127]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-127 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, on request, vary in respect of a vessel a requirement or
  condition made under paragraph 136(1)(b) or (c) if the Minister is
  satisfied that the varied requirement or condition would result in an
  equivalent or greater level of safety.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A contravention of a requirement that is varied under
  subsection (1) is deemed to be a contravention of the original
  requirement.

### [Aids to Navigation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52106 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Aids to navigation vest in Her Majesty

**[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** All aids to navigation
acquired, installed, built or maintained at the expense of a province
before it became a part of Canada, or at the expense of the Government
of Canada, and all buildings and other works relating to those aids, are
vested in Her Majesty in right of Canada and are under the control and
management of the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to report damage

- **[[129]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-129 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  vessel, or anything towed by a vessel, runs down, moves, damages or
  destroys an aid to navigation in Canadian waters, the person in charge
  of the vessel shall, without delay, make a report to a marine
  communications and traffic services officer or, if that is not
  feasible, to an officer of the Canadian Coast Guard.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to report --- navigation
  hazard

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person in charge of a vessel in Canadian waters who
  discovers an uncharted hazard to navigation, or discovers that an aid
  to navigation is missing, out of position or malfunctioning, shall
  make a report without delay to a marine communications and traffic
  services officer or, if that is not feasible, to an officer of the
  Canadian Coast Guard.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 129
- [2023, c. 26, s. 378(F)](#1410339-1413822){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410339-1413822.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-129-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 129"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Search and Rescue]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52114 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation --- search and rescue mission
coordinators

- **[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may designate persons as search and rescue mission
  coordinators to organize search and rescue operations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers --- search and rescue mission
  coordinators

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On being informed that a person, a vessel or an
  aircraft is in distress or is missing in Canadian waters, in the
  exclusive economic zone of Canada or on the high seas off any of the
  coasts of Canada under circumstances that indicate that they may be in
  distress, a search and rescue mission coordinator may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} direct all vessels within an area that the search
    and rescue mission coordinator specifies to report their positions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct any vessel to take part in a search for that
    person, vessel or aircraft or to otherwise render assistance;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} give any other directions that the search and
    rescue mission coordinator considers necessary to carry out search
    and rescue operations for that person, vessel or aircraft; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} use any lands if it is necessary to do so for the
    purpose of saving the life of a shipwrecked person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to comply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every vessel or person on board a vessel in Canadian
  waters and every vessel or person on board a vessel in any waters that
  has a master who is a qualified person shall comply with a direction
  given to it or them under subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 130
- 2018, c. 27, s. 695
- [2023, c. 26, s. 379](#1410341-1413824){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410341-1413824.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-130-20181213.html "Link to previous version of section 130"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Answering distress signal

- **[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, the master
  of a vessel in Canadian waters and every qualified person who is the
  master of a vessel in any waters, on receiving a signal from any
  source that a person, a vessel or an aircraft is in distress, shall
  proceed with all speed to render assistance and shall, if possible,
  inform the persons in distress or the sender of the signal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distress signal --- no assistance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the master is unable or, in the special
  circumstances of the case, considers it unreasonable or unnecessary to
  proceed to the assistance of a person, a vessel or an aircraft in
  distress, the master is not required to proceed to their assistance
  and is to enter the reason in the official log book of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ships requisitioned

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The master of any vessel in distress may requisition
  one or more of any vessels that answer the distress call to render
  assistance. The master of a requisitioned vessel in Canadian waters
  and every qualified person who is the master of a requisitioned vessel
  in any waters shall continue to proceed with all speed to render
  assistance to the vessel in distress.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release from obligation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The master of a vessel shall be released from the
  obligation imposed by subsection (1) when the master learns that
  another vessel is complying with a requisition referred to in
  subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further release

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The master of a vessel shall be released from an
  obligation imposed by subsection (1) or (3) if the master is informed
  by the persons in distress or by the master of another vessel that has
  reached those persons that assistance is no longer necessary.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance

**[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** The master of a vessel
in Canadian waters and every qualified person who is the master of a
vessel in any waters shall render assistance to every person who is
found at sea and in danger of being lost.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Aircraft treated as if vessel

**[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** Sections 130 to 132
apply in respect of aircraft on or over Canadian waters as they apply in
respect of vessels in Canadian waters, with any modifications that the
circumstances require.

**[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2013, c.
28, s. 9\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-134-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 134"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Enforcement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52144 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

- **[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may designate any person or class of persons for the purposes
  of enforcing this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation --- Minister of Transport

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Transport may designate any person
  or class of persons for the purposes of enforcing a provision of this
  Part or of a regulation made under this Part that the Minister of
  Transport is responsible for administering.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stopping and boarding vessel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person, or a member of a class of persons,
  designated under subsection (1) or (1.1) who has reasonable grounds to
  believe that an offence has been committed or is about to be committed
  under this Part by a vessel or any person on board a vessel may stop
  and board the vessel and take any reasonable action to ensure public
  safety or protect the public interest.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 135
- [2023, c. 26, s. 380](#1410343-1413831){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410343-1413831.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-135-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 135"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52150 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Minister of Transport

- **[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister of
  Transport, make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} establishing VTS Zones within Canadian waters or in
    a shipping safety control zone prescribed under the [Arctic Waters
    Pollution Prevention Act](/eng/acts/A-12);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the information to be provided and the
    procedures and practices to be followed by vessels that are about to
    enter, leave or proceed within a VTS Zone;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the conditions under which a clearance
    under section 126 is to be granted;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} defining the expression [[about to
    enter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for the purpose of this Part;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting aids to navigation in Canadian waters;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} regulating or prohibiting the navigation,
    anchoring, mooring or berthing of vessels for the purposes of
    promoting the safe and efficient navigation of vessels and
    protecting the public interest and the environment;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} respecting the safety of persons on Canadian waters
    for the purposes of sporting, recreational or public events or
    activities;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} specifying classes of persons, or appointing
    persons, to ensure compliance with regulations made under any of
    paragraphs (b) and (e) to (g) and specifying their powers and
    duties; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that may be prescribed under
    this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister, make regulations

  - [(a) and (b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2013, c. 28, s.
    10\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting maritime search and rescue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order --- Minister of Transport

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Transport may, by order, suspend or
  modify the operation of regulations made under paragraph (1)(f) or
  (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of effect of order

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (3) has effect from
  the time that it is made or from any later date that may be specified
  in the order but ceases to have effect on the earliest of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which it is repealed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a regulation made under this Act
    that has the same effect as the order comes into force; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} two years after the effective date of the order or
    any shorter period that may be specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to an order made under subsection (3). However, the order
  must be published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
  within 23 days after it is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 136
- 2005, c. 29, s. 18
- 2013, c. 28, s. 10
- [2023, c. 26, s. 381](#1410345-1413841){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410345-1413841.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-136-20131201.html "Link to previous version of section 136"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52169 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act

- **[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that,
  contravenes any of the following commits an offence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 131(1) (assist persons in distress);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 131(3) (comply with requisition to
    assist person in distress); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} section 132 (assist a person found at sea).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that, commits an offence
  under subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not
  more than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than
  18 months, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person on board a vessel may be convicted of an
  offence under paragraph (1)(a), (b) or (c) if they had reasonable
  grounds to believe that compliance with subsection 131(1) or (3) or
  section 132, as the case may be, would have imperilled life, the
  vessel or another vessel.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that,
  contravenes any of the following commits an offence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 126(1)(a) (entering, leaving or
    proceeding within a VTS Zone without a clearance);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 126(1)(b) (proceeding within a VTS Zone
    when unable to maintain direct communication);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 126(3)(b), (c)
    or (d) (to provide information, to use radio frequencies or to
    leave, refrain from entering, proceed to or remain in a VTS Zone);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 126(5)(a) (take all reasonable measures
    to communicate);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 126(5)(b) (obtain clearance);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 126(6) (remain at port or proceed to
    safe port);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} subsection 129(1) (report disturbance of aid to
    navigation);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} subsection 129(2) (report navigation hazard);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 130(3) (comply with direction of search
    and rescue mission coordinator); or

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under this
    Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that, commits an offence
  under subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not
  more than \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than one
  year, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defence available in certain cases

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No vessel or person on board a vessel may be
  convicted of contravening subsection 126(1) (entering, leaving or
  proceeding within a VTS Zone) or a provision of the regulations made
  under paragraph 136(1)(b) if they had reasonable grounds to believe
  that compliance with that provision would have imperilled life, the
  vessel, another vessel or any property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vessel may be detained

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister or a person authorized by the
  Minister for the purpose of this subsection believes on reasonable
  grounds that an offence referred to in subsection (1) has been
  committed by or in respect of a vessel, the Minister or that person
  may make a detention order in respect of the vessel, and section 222
  (detention of vessels) applies to the detention order, with any
  modifications that the circumstances require.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 138
- 2018, c. 27, s. 696
:::

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Transport.

### [Obligations in Case of Collisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52251 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of masters in collision

**[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** If vessels collide, the
master or person in charge of each vessel shall, if and in so far as
they can to do so without endangering their vessel, crew or passengers,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} render to the other vessel, its master, crew and
  passengers the assistance that may be necessary to save them from any
  danger caused by the collision, and to stay by the other vessel until
  the master or person has determined that it has no need of further
  assistance; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} give the name of their vessel, if any, the name and
  address of its authorized representative, if any, and any other
  prescribed information to the master or person in charge of the other
  vessel.

### [Inquiry into Causes of Death]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52256 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiry into cause of death on board

- **[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person dies on board a Canadian vessel, on the vessel's arrival at a
  port in Canada, the Minister is to endeavour to ascertain the cause of
  death and, for that purpose, may hold an inquiry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of the inquiry, the Minister has the
  powers of a commissioner under Part I of the [Inquiries
  Act](/eng/acts/I-11) and may, if it appears to the Minister to be
  necessary for the purpose of the inquiry,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} go on board any vessel and inspect it or any part
    of it, or any of its machinery, equipment or cargo, while not
    detaining the vessel for longer than necessary from proceeding on a
    voyage; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} enter and inspect any premises at any reasonable
    time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant required to enter living
  quarters

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Living quarters may not be entered under
  subsection (2) unless they are entered with the consent of the
  occupant or under the authority of a warrant issued under subsection
  (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to issue warrant

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application, a
  justice, within the meaning of section 2 of the [Criminal
  Code](/eng/acts/C-46), may issue a warrant authorizing the Minister to
  enter living quarters, subject to any conditions that may be specified
  in the warrant, if the justice is satisfied by information on oath
  that entry to the living quarters

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is necessary for the purpose of an inquiry; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has been refused or there are reasonable grounds
    for believing that it will be refused.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of force

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In executing a warrant, the Minister may not use
  force unless the Minister is accompanied by a peace officer and the
  use of force has been specifically authorized in the warrant.

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52272 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Minister

- **[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make
  regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the reporting of accidents or dangerous
    occurrences happening to or on board vessels, whether or not
    attended with loss of life;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2019, c. 1, s. 144\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the use of photographs, film, video
    recordings or electronic images of the human remains of victims of
    accidents involving a wrecked vessel or an aircraft wrecked in
    waters; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that may be prescribed under
    this Part.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2019, c. 1, s. 144\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote

- 2005, c. 2, s. 8

  role="button"}
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52284 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of paragraph 148(a) or the
regulations

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person commits an offence who
  contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 148(a) (render assistance after a
    collision); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under paragraph
    150(1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than 18
  months, or to both.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person commits an offence who
  contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 148(b) (failure to provide information
    after a collision); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under paragraph
    150(1)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$25,000.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 152
- [2019, c. 1, s. 145](#1114281-1196984){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1114281-1196984.html"
  role="button"}
- [2023, c. 26, s. 382](#1410347-1413844){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410347-1413844.html"
  role="button"}
