## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Pollution Prevention and Response --- Department of Transport and Department of Fisheries and Oceans]{.HTitleText1} {#h-52393 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52394 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[discharge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[discharge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a discharge of a pollutant
    from a vessel, or a discharge of oil from an oil handling facility
    or of a hazardous and noxious substance from a hazardous and noxious
    substances handling facility if the facility is engaged in loading
    to or unloading from a vessel, that directly or indirectly results
    in the pollutant entering the water, and includes spilling, leaking,
    pumping, pouring, emitting, emptying, throwing and
    dumping. ([rejet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hazardous and noxious substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazardous and noxious substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    substance other than oil that, if introduced into the marine
    environment, is likely to create hazards to human health, harm
    living resources and marine life, damage amenities or interfere with
    legitimate uses of the marine environment. ([substance nocive et
    potentiellement dangereuse)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}

[[hazardous and noxious substances pollution incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazardous and noxious substances pollution
    incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any occurrence or series of
    occurrences having the same origin, including fire or explosion,
    that results or may result in a discharge of hazardous and noxious
    substances. ([événement de pollution par les substances nocives et
    potentiellement dangereuses]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
    Transport. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means petroleum in any form, including
    crude oil, fuel oil, sludge, oil refuse and refined
    products. ([hydrocarbures]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[oil pollution incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[oil pollution incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an occurrence,
    or a series of occurrences having the same origin, that results or
    may result in a discharge of oil. ([événement de pollution par les
    hydrocarbures]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pollutant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pollutant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a substance that, if added to any waters, would
      degrade or alter or form part of a process of degradation or
      alteration of the quality of the waters to an extent that is
      detrimental to their use by humans or by an animal or a plant that
      is useful to humans; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any water that contains a substance in such a
      quantity or concentration, or that has been so treated, processed
      or changed, by heat or other means, from a natural state, that it
      would, if added to any waters, degrade or alter or form part of a
      process of degradation or alteration of the quality of the waters
      to an extent that is detrimental to their use by humans or by an
      animal or a plant that is useful to humans.

    It includes oil, hazardous and noxious substances and any substance
    or class of substances that is prescribed for the purpose of this
    Part to be a pollutant. ([polluant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pollution damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pollution damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in relation to a vessel, an
    oil handling facility or a hazardous and noxious substances
    facility, means loss or damage outside the vessel or facility caused
    by contamination resulting from a discharge from the vessel or
    facility. ([dommages dus à la pollution]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[response organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[response organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a qualified
    person to whom the Minister issues a certificate of designation
    under subsection 169(1). ([organisme
    d'intervention]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 165
- 2005, c. 29, s. 21
- 2018, c. 27, s. 698
- [2023, c. 26, s. 383](#1410349-1413853){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410349-1413853.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-165-20181213.html "Link to previous version of section 165"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52409 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this
  Part, this Part applies in respect of vessels in Canadian waters or
  waters in the exclusive economic zone of Canada and in respect of oil
  handling facilities and hazardous and noxious substances handling
  facilities that are in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply in respect of a vessel that
  is on location and engaged in the exploration or drilling for, or the
  production, conservation or processing of, oil or gas in an area
  described in paragraph 3(a) or (b) of the [Canada Oil and Gas
  Operations Act](/eng/acts/O-7).

- Definition of [[oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
  [[gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (2), [[oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
  [[gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meaning as in section 2 of
  the [Canada Oil and Gas Operations Act](/eng/acts/O-7).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 166
- [2023, c. 26, s. 384](#1410354-1413857){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410354-1413857.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-166-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 166"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Discharges of Oil]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52417 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vessels --- requirements

- **[[167]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), every
  prescribed vessel or vessel of a prescribed class shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have an arrangement with a response organization in
    respect of a quantity of oil that is at least equal to the total
    amount of oil that the vessel carries, both as cargo and as fuel, to
    a prescribed maximum quantity, and in respect of waters where the
    vessel navigates or engages in a marine activity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have on board a declaration, in the form specified
    by the Minister, that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} identifies the name and address of the vessel's
      insurer or, in the case of a subscription policy, the name and
      address of the lead insurer who provides pollution insurance
      coverage in respect of the vessel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} confirms that the arrangement has been made, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} identifies every person who is authorized to
      implement the arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain provisions do not apply to
  certain vessels

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) and subparagraphs (1)(b)(ii)
  and (iii) do not apply in respect of a vessel that is in prescribed
  waters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may exempt, subject to any conditions
  that the Minister considers appropriate, for a specified period any
  vessel, or class of vessels, that is en route through waters referred
  to in subsection 166(1), from the application of any provision of this
  Part if the Minister is of the opinion that the vessel or class of
  vessels is subject to a provision of the laws of another state that
  provides for standards that are equivalent to or stricter than the
  standards provided for in the provision of this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notice of every exemption must be published in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 167
- 2005, c. 29, s. 22
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-167-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 167"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Oil Handling Facilities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1185908 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of proposed operations

**[[167.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167.1 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, a person who proposes to operate an oil handling facility
of a class established by the regulations shall, within the prescribed
time, notify the Minister of the proposed operations relating to the
loading or unloading of oil to or from vessels and shall submit to the
Minister any information or documents required by the regulations and,
within the time specified by the Minister, any information or documents
requested by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2014, c. 29, s. 60](#1035562-1185912){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1035562-1185912.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of plans

- **[[167.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, a person
  who proposes to operate an oil handling facility of a class
  established by the regulations shall, at least 90 days before the day
  on which the oil handling facility's operations relating to the
  loading or unloading of oil to or from vessels will begin --- or
  within any other time specified by the Minister --- submit to the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an oil pollution prevention plan to prevent a
    discharge of oil during the loading or unloading of a vessel, which
    meets the requirements set out in the regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an oil pollution emergency plan to respond to a
    discharge of oil during the loading or unloading of a vessel, which
    meets the requirements set out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of information or documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person referred to in subsection (1) shall submit
  to the Minister any information or documents requested by the
  Minister, within the time specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition against beginning
  operations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person referred to in subsection (1) shall not
  begin operations relating to the loading or unloading of oil to or
  from vessels unless the plans submitted under subsection (1) meet the
  requirements set out in the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2014, c. 29, s. 60](#1035562-1185923){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1035562-1185923.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of operations

**[[167.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167.3 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, the operator of an oil handling facility of a class
established by the regulations shall notify the Minister of the oil
handling facility's operations relating to the loading or unloading of
oil to or from vessels within 90 days after the day on which this
section comes into force and shall submit to the Minister any
information or documents required by the regulations and, within the
time specified by the Minister, any information or documents requested
by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2014, c. 29, s. 60](#1035562-1185927){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1035562-1185927.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of plans

**[[167.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167.4 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, unless the plans have already been submitted under
subsection 167.2(1), the operator of an oil handling facility of a class
established by the regulations shall submit to the Minister, within the
time set out in the regulations, an oil pollution prevention plan to
prevent a discharge of oil during the loading or unloading of a vessel
and an oil pollution emergency plan to respond to a discharge of oil
during the loading or unloading of a vessel --- which meet the
requirements set out in the regulations --- and shall submit to the
Minister any information or documents requested by the Minister, within
the time specified by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2014, c. 29, s. 60](#1035562-1185931){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1035562-1185931.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Oil handling facilities --- requirements

- **[[168]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the
  operator of an oil handling facility of a class established by the
  regulations shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have an arrangement with a response organization in
    respect of any quantity of oil that is, at any time, involved in
    being loaded or unloaded to or from a vessel at the oil handling
    facility, to a prescribed maximum quantity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have on site a declaration in the form specified by
    the Minister that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} describes the manner in which the operator will
      comply with the regulations made under paragraph 182(1)(a),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} confirms that the arrangement has been made, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} identifies every person who is authorized to
      implement the arrangement and the oil pollution emergency plan
      referred to in paragraph (d);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have on site an up-to-date oil pollution prevention
    plan to prevent a discharge of oil during the loading or unloading
    of a vessel, which meets the requirements set out in the
    regulations;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} submit the up-to-date oil pollution prevention
    plan to the Minister within the time and in the circumstances set
    out in the regulations;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} have on site an up-to-date oil pollution emergency
    plan to respond to a discharge or possible discharge of oil during
    the loading or unloading of a vessel, which meets the requirements
    set out in the regulations;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} submit the up-to-date oil pollution emergency
    plan to the Minister within the time and in the circumstances set
    out in the regulations; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} have the procedures, equipment and resources
    required by the regulations available for immediate use in the event
    of a discharge of oil during the loading or unloading of a vessel.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2014, c. 29, s. 61\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to take reasonable measures ---
  oil handling facilities

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator of an oil handling facility referred to
  in subsection (1) shall take reasonable measures to implement

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the oil pollution prevention plan referred to in
    paragraph (1)(c); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an oil pollution incident, the oil
    pollution emergency plan referred to in paragraph (1)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 168
- [2014, c. 29, s. 61](#1035574-1185942){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1035574-1185942.html"
  role="button"}
- [2023, c. 26, s. 389](#1410367-1413859){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410367-1413859.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-168-20190622.html "Link to previous version of section 168"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of proposed change to
operations

- **[[168.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, an
  operator of an oil handling facility of a class established by the
  regulations that proposes to make a change, or permit a change to be
  made, to the oil handling facility's operations relating to the
  loading or unloading of oil to or from vessels shall --- at least 180
  days before the day on which it makes the change or permits the change
  to be made --- notify the Minister of the change, including any of the
  following changes:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change in the oil handling facility's transfer
    rate, if the change would result in the oil handling facility
    becoming part of a different class established by the regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change in the design of the oil handling
    facility, or a change in the oil handling facility's equipment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a change in the type or composition of oil that is
    loaded or unloaded to or from vessels.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of information or documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator referred to in subsection (1) shall
  submit to the Minister any information or documents required by the
  regulations and, within the time specified by the Minister, any
  information or documents requested by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revise plans

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator referred to in subsection (1) shall
  revise the oil pollution prevention plan and the oil pollution
  emergency plan and submit the revised plans to the Minister at least
  90 days before making the change or permitting the change to be made,
  or within any other time specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition against making changes

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An operator shall not make a change referred to in
  subsection (1) or permit one to be made unless the plans submitted
  under subsection (3) meet the requirements set out in the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2014, c. 29, s. 62](#1035584-1185956){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1035584-1185956.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Update or revise plans

**[[168.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168.1 .sectionLabel}** Despite any other
provision of this Part or the regulations, the Minister may direct the
operator of an oil handling facility to update or revise an oil
pollution prevention plan or an oil pollution emergency plan and to
submit the up-to-date or revised plan to the Minister within the time
specified by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 29, s. 63
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provide information

**[[168.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168.2 .sectionLabel}** A marine safety
inspector may direct any person to provide the inspector with any
information that the inspector reasonably requires in the administration
of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 29, s. 63
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may take measures

**[[168.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168.3 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister
believes on reasonable grounds that an oil handling facility has
discharged, is discharging or may discharge oil, that the oil pollution
prevention plan or the oil pollution emergency plan for an oil handling
facility does not meet the requirements set out in the regulations or
that the operator of an oil handling facility does not have the
procedures, equipment and resources required by the regulations
available for immediate use in the event of a discharge of oil during
the loading or unloading of a vessel, the Minister may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} monitor the measures taken by any person to repair,
  remedy, minimize or prevent pollution damage from the oil handling
  facility; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if he or she considers it necessary, direct the
  operator of the oil handling facility to take the measures that the
  Minister considers necessary to repair, remedy, minimize or prevent
  pollution damage from the oil handling facility, including to stop
  loading or unloading oil to or from vessels.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 29, s. 63
- 2018, c. 27, s. 699
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-168.3-20141209.html "Link to previous version of section 168.3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Response Organizations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52465 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of designation

- **[[169]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-169 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, in respect of any geographic area and in respect of a
  prescribed quantity of oil, issue a certificate of designation as a
  response organization to a qualified person who makes an application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application for a certificate must be made in the
  form and manner, include the information and be accompanied by the
  documents specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further evidence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the specified information and
  documents, the Minister may require that an applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide evidence, including declarations, that the
    Minister considers necessary to establish that the requirements for
    the issuance of the certificate have been met; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} undergo any examinations and have its installations
    undergo any inspections that the Minister considers necessary to
    establish that the requirements for the issuance of the certificate
    have been met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every certificate is valid for the period specified
  by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue or renew

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may refuse to issue or renew a
  certificate if the Minister is of the opinion that the public interest
  and, in particular, the record of the applicant or of a principal of
  the applicant warrant it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension and cancellation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may suspend or cancel a certificate in
  the circumstances and on the grounds set out in the regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement of fees

- **[[170]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  response organization, or a qualified person who makes an application
  under subsection 169(1), must notify the Minister, in the form and
  manner and including the information and accompanied by the documents
  specified by the Minister, of the fees that they propose to charge in
  relation to an arrangement referred to in paragraph 167(1)(a) or
  168(1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A response organization, or a qualified person who
  makes an application under subsection 169(1), must give notice of the
  proposed fees in the prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charging fees

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A response organization may not charge the fees
  before the expiry of 30 days after the notice is given.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee review

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On the application of any interested person in the
  prescribed manner within 30 days after the notice is given, the
  Minister is to review the reasonableness of the proposed fees.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may appoint a person to assist in the
  review. The person has all the powers of a commissioner under Part I
  of the [Inquiries Act](/eng/acts/I-11).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to amend or eliminate fee

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by order, amend or eliminate a fee
  reviewed under subsection (4). The order comes into effect on the
  first day that the fee is charged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of order

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The response organization affected by the order must
  give notice of it in the prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed procedures, equipment and
resources

**[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171 .sectionLabel}** Every response
organization shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} have a response plan that meets the prescribed
  requirements;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} have the prescribed equipment and resources at the
  site set out in the response plan;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} provide or arrange for prescribed training to
  prescribed classes of persons;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} undertake and participate in prescribed activities to
  evaluate the response plan or its implementation;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} on the request of a vessel or the operator of an oil
  handling facility with which the response organization has an
  arrangement referred to in paragraph 167(1)(a) or 168(1)(a), as the
  case may be, implement a response consistent with the response plan;
  and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} on the request of the Minister or an advisory council
  established under section 172, provide information regarding any of
  the matters referred to in paragraphs (a) to (e).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provide documents

**[[171.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171.1 .sectionLabel}** A marine safety
inspector may direct a response organization to provide the inspector
with any document that the organization is required to have under this
Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 29, s. 64
:::

### [Advisory Councils]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52508 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advisory councils

- **[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may establish an advisory council in respect of any
  geographic area for the purpose of advising the Minister with respect
  to this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each advisory council is to be composed of no more
  than seven members who are appointed to the council by the Minister
  and who, in the Minister's opinion, can represent the communities and
  interests potentially affected by an oil spill in that area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each member of an advisory council is to be appointed
  for a term of not more than three years and is eligible for
  reappointment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}President

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An advisory council must elect one of its members to
  be its president.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Honoraria and expenses

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The members of an advisory council may be paid the
  honoraria that the Minister considers appropriate and may be paid any
  reasonable travel, living and child care expenses incurred by them
  when engaged on the business of the council while absent from their
  ordinary place of residence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recommendations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An advisory council is to advise and may make
  recommendations to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meetings in public

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Advisory council meetings must be open to the public
  unless the council is satisfied that a public meeting would not be in
  the public interest, in which case the meeting or any part of it may
  be held in private.

### [Report to Parliament]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52524 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review and report by Minister

**[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** Every five years, the
Minister must review the operation of sections 167 to 172 and have laid
before each House of Parliament a report setting out the results of the
review.

### [Pollution Response Officers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52527 .Subheading}

**[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2014, c.
29, s. 65\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-174-20051005.html "Link to previous version of section 174"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of pollution response officer

- **[[174.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Fisheries and Oceans
  may designate any persons or classes of persons as pollution response
  officers in respect of discharges or threats of discharges and may
  limit in any manner that he or she considers appropriate the powers
  that the officers may exercise under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of designation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Fisheries and Oceans must furnish
  every pollution response officer with a certificate of designation
  and, if the officer's powers are limited under subsection (1), the
  certificate must specify the powers that the officer may exercise.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 27, s. 700\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 29, s. 23
- 2018, c. 27, s. 700
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-174.1-20051005.html "Link to previous version of section 174.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2014, c.
29, s. 66\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-175-20051005.html "Link to previous version of section 175"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of pollution response officers ---
general

- **[[175.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A pollution response officer may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} direct a vessel, if it is about to enter or is
    within waters in respect of which this Part applies, to provide him
    or her with any information that he or she considers appropriate for
    the administration of this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct any vessel that is within or about to enter
    waters in respect of which this Part applies and that he or she
    believes on reasonable grounds to be carrying a pollutant to proceed
    through those waters by the route, and at a speed not in excess of
    the speed, that he or she may specify;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} direct a vessel that is required to have a
    shipboard oil pollution emergency plan under the regulations to
    provide information concerning it and its implementation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} direct the operator of an oil handling facility to
    provide any document that the operator is required to have on site
    under this Part; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} direct a response organization to provide any
    document that the organization is required to have under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers --- discharge of pollutant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the pollution response officer believes on
  reasonable grounds that a vessel has discharged, is discharging or may
  discharge a pollutant, he or she may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} direct a vessel that is within or about to enter
    waters in respect of which this Part applies to proceed through
    those waters by the route, and at a speed not in excess of the
    speed, that he or she may specify;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} go on board and take samples of any substance that
    he or she believes to be the pollutant;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the vessel is within or about to enter waters in
    respect of which this Part applies, direct the vessel to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} proceed to the place within waters in respect of
      which this Part applies that he or she may specify, by the route
      and in the manner that he or she may specify, and to moor, anchor
      or remain there for any reasonable period that he or she may
      specify,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} proceed out of waters in respect of which this
      Part applies, by the route and in the manner that he or she may
      specify, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} remain outside waters in respect of which this
      Part applies; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if he or she is informed that a substantial
    quantity of a pollutant has entered or been discharged in waters in
    respect of which this Part applies, or if on reasonable grounds he
    or she is satisfied that a grave and imminent danger of a
    substantial discharge of a pollutant in those waters exists, declare
    an emergency zone, the size of which is reasonable with regard to
    the seriousness of the situation, and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} direct any vessel within that emergency zone to
      report its position to him or her,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} direct any vessel not to enter or not to leave
      the emergency zone, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} direct any vessel within the emergency zone in
      respect of routes, speed limits and pilotage and equipment
      requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of samples

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An officer who takes a sample under paragraph (2)(b)
  may dispose of it in any manner that he or she considers appropriate
  or may submit it for analysis or examination to a person designated by
  the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate or report

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person who has made an analysis or examination may
  issue a certificate or report that sets out the results of the
  analysis or examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (6) and (7), the certificate
  or report is admissible in evidence in any proceeding related to an
  offence under this Part and, in the absence of any evidence to the
  contrary, is proof of the statements contained in the certificate or
  report without proof of the signature or the official character of the
  person appearing to have signed it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attendance of person

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The party against whom the certificate or report is
  produced may, with leave of the court, require for the purposes of
  cross-examination the attendance of the person who issued it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The certificate or report may be admitted in evidence
  only if the party who intends to produce it has given to the party
  against whom it is intended to be produced reasonable notice of that
  intention, together with a copy of the certificate or report.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 29, s. 23
- 2018, c. 27, s. 701
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-175.1-20051005.html "Link to previous version of section 175.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of pollution response officer

- **[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of exercising his or her powers under this Part, a
  pollution response officer may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} board any vessel or enter any premises or other
    place at any reasonable time;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct any person to provide reasonable assistance
    or put into operation or cease operating any machinery or equipment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} direct any person to provide any information that
    the officer may reasonably require in the administration of this
    Part;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} direct any person to produce for inspection, or for
    the purpose of making copies or taking extracts, any log book or
    other document;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} take photographs and make video recordings and
    sketches;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} use or cause to be used any computer system or data
    processing system at the place to examine any data contained in, or
    available to, the system;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} reproduce or cause to be reproduced any record from
    the data in the form of a print-out or other intelligible output;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} take any document or other thing from the place
    where the inspection is being carried out for examination or, in the
    case of a document, copying; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} use or cause to be used any copying equipment in
    the place where the inspection is being carried out to make copies
    of any documents.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Living quarters may not be entered under
  subsection (1) unless they are entered with the consent of the
  occupant or under the authority of a warrant issued under subsection
  (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to issue warrant

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application, a
  justice of the peace may issue a warrant authorizing a pollution
  response officer to enter living quarters, subject to any conditions
  that may be specified in the warrant, if the justice is satisfied by
  information on oath that entry to the living quarters

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is necessary for any purpose related to the
    carrying out of the officer's powers under this Part; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has been refused or there are reasonable grounds
    for believing that it will be refused.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of force

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No officer executing a warrant may use force unless
  they are accompanied by a peace officer and the use of force is
  specifically authorized in the warrant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of documents and things

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Documents or other things taken under paragraph
  (1)(h) must be returned as soon as feasible after they are no longer
  required for the inspection or for any proceedings that may result
  from it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 176
- 2005, c. 29, s. 24
- 2014, c. 29, s. 67
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-176-20051005.html "Link to previous version of section 176"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Detention of Vessels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52594 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention

- **[[177]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-177 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  pollution response officer believes on reasonable grounds that an
  offence under this Part has been committed by or in respect of a
  vessel, he or she may make a detention order in respect of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to be in writing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A detention order made under this section must be in
  writing and be addressed to every person empowered to grant clearance
  in respect of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention order to be served on master

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notice of a detention order made under this section
  in respect of a vessel must be served on the master

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by delivering a copy of the notice personally to
    the master; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if service cannot reasonably be effected in the
    manner provided in paragraph (a), by leaving a copy of the notice
    with the person who is, or appears to be, in charge of the vessel
    or, if there is no such person, by fixing a copy of the notice to a
    prominent part of the vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The notice must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} indicate the measures to ensure compliance with
    this Part that must be taken for the detention order to be
    rescinded; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an indictment has been preferred in respect of
    the offence, indicate the amount and form of security that, pending
    the outcome of any proceedings related to the indictment, must be
    deposited with the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans for the
    detention order to be rescinded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Foreign state to be notified

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a vessel in respect of which a detention order is
  made under this section is registered in a foreign state, that state
  is to be notified that the order was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rescission of orders

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A pollution response officer must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} rescind a detention order made under this section
    if he or she is satisfied that the measures indicated in the notice
    referred to in subsection (4) have been taken and, if applicable,
    security in the amount and form indicated in the notice referred to
    in that subsection has been deposited with the Minister of Fisheries
    and Oceans; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify, in the form and manner specified by the
    Minister of Fisheries and Oceans, the master and the persons
    referred to in subsection (2) of the rescission.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of persons empowered to give
  clearance

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} No person to whom a detention order made under this
  section is addressed shall, after notice of the order is received by
  them, grant clearance to the vessel in respect of which the order was
  made unless they have been notified that the order has been rescinded
  under subsection (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Movement of vessel prohibited

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 179, no person shall move a vessel
  that is subject to a detention order made under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for expenses

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative or, if there is no
  authorized representative, the owner of a vessel that is detained
  under this section is liable for all expenses incurred in respect of
  the detained vessel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of security

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Fisheries and Oceans, after
  proceedings in respect of which security was deposited are concluded,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may apply the security to reimburse Her Majesty in
    right of Canada, either fully or partially, if any of the expenses
    or any fine has not been paid; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is to return the security, or any part of it that
    remains if it is applied under paragraph (a), if all expenses and
    any fine imposed have been paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 177
- 2005, c. 29, s. 25
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-177-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 177"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interference with service

**[[178]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-178 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
wilfully interfere with the service of a notice of a detention order.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to move a detained vessel

**[[179]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-179 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of
Fisheries and Oceans may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} on application made by the authorized representative
  or, if there is no authorized representative, the owner of a detained
  vessel, in the form and manner specified by the Minister of Fisheries
  and Oceans, permit the master to move it in accordance with the
  directions of the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} on application made by the owner of a dock or wharf,
  or by the person in charge of a harbour, at which a detained vessel is
  situated, in the form and manner specified by the Minister of
  Fisheries and Oceans, direct the person who is, or appears to be, in
  charge of the vessel to move the vessel in accordance with the
  directions of the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a person to whom a direction is given under
  paragraph (b) does not comply with it and the Minister of Fisheries
  and Oceans is satisfied that the applicant for the direction has
  sufficient insurance in place to cover any incident that may arise
  from the moving of the vessel, authorize the applicant to move the
  vessel in accordance with the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans'
  directions and at the expense of the authorized representative or, if
  there is no authorized representative, the owner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 179
- 2005, c. 29, s. 26
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-179-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 179"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Response Measures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52635 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister of Fisheries and Oceans ---
measures

- **[[180]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans believes on reasonable grounds
  that a vessel, an oil handling facility or a hazardous and noxious
  substances handling facility has discharged, is discharging or may
  discharge a pollutant, that Minister may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take the measures that that Minister considers
    necessary to repair, remedy, minimize or prevent pollution damage
    from the vessel or facility, including, in the case of a vessel, by
    removing --- or by selling, dismantling, destroying or otherwise
    disposing of --- the vessel or its contents;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} monitor the measures taken by any person or vessel
    to repair, remedy, minimize or prevent pollution damage from the
    vessel or facility; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if that Minister considers it necessary to do so,
    direct any person or vessel to take measures referred to in
    paragraph (a) or to refrain from doing so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clear title

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a vessel or its contents are disposed of under
  paragraph 180(1)(a), the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans may give the
  person acquiring them a valid title to the vessel or its contents free
  from any mortgage, hypothec, maritime lien or other interest or right
  that is in existence at the time of the disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}At risk and expense of owner

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The disposition is at the risk, cost and expense of
  the owner of the vessel or its contents.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of proceeds

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} When a vessel or its contents are disposed of under
  paragraph 180(1)(a), any surplus remaining from the proceeds of the
  disposition after deducting the costs and expenses incurred in respect
  of the disposition shall be paid to the Minister of Fisheries and
  Oceans, in respect of all costs and expenses incurred in taking any
  other measures under this Part, and to the holders, if known at the
  time of the disposition, of any mortgages, hypothecs, maritime liens
  or other interests or rights that are in existence at the time of the
  disposition, and any amount that remains after paying the Minister of
  Fisheries and Oceans and the holders shall be paid to the owner of the
  vessel or the contents.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal Court directions

  [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Fisheries and Oceans may apply to
  the Federal Court for directions as to the allocation of the surplus
  referred to in subsection (2.2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Compensation must be paid by His Majesty in right of
  Canada for the services of any person or vessel, other than the
  operator of a vessel, an oil handling facility or a hazardous and
  noxious substances handling facility that had discharged, was
  discharging or may have discharged a pollutant, that has complied with
  a direction issued under paragraph (1)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Priority

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A direction made by the Minister of Fisheries and
  Oceans under paragraph (1)(c) prevails over an order or direction made
  under any Act of Parliament, to the extent of any inconsistency.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 180
- 2005, c. 29, s. 27
- 2018, c. 27, s. 702
- [2023, c. 26, s. 394](#1410385-1413866){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410385-1413866.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-180-20181213.html "Link to previous version of section 180"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry on private property

- **[[180.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Fisheries and Oceans
  or a pollution response officer may enter private property -- other
  than a dwelling-house -- and pass through it, including with vehicles
  and equipment, for the purposes of exercising their powers or
  performing their duties or functions under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accompanying persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans or a
  pollution response officer enters private property and passes through
  it, they may be accompanied by any person who they believe is
  necessary to help them in exercising their powers or performing their
  duties or functions under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of property

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If required, the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans or
  the pollution response officer may use property adjacent to or in the
  vicinity of a vessel, oil handling facility or hazardous and noxious
  substances handling facility --- other than a dwelling-house --- for
  the purposes of exercising their powers or performing their duties or
  functions under this Part, and any person accompanying that Minister
  or the pollution response officer may use such property to help that
  Minister or officer exercise their powers or perform their duties or
  functions under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada may compensate the
  owner of the property referred to in subsection (3), or any person
  that has, either by law or by contract, the rights of the owner of
  that property in respect of its possession and use, for any loss or
  damage caused by the use of that property under subsection (3) that
  exceeds the value of the benefit derived by the owner or person from
  that use.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 27, s. 703
- [2023, c. 26, s. 395](#1410392-1413869){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410392-1413869.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-180.1-20181213.html "Link to previous version of section 180.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

**[[180.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180.2 .sectionLabel}** An order or
direction given under this Part by the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans
or a pollution response officer is not a [[statutory
instrument]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in the [Statutory
Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 27, s. 703
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity --- taking or refraining from
taking measures

- **[[181]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who, or vessel that, is directed to take or refrain from taking
  measures under paragraph 180(1)(c) is not personally liable, either
  civilly or criminally, for anything they do or omit to do in the
  course of complying with the direction, unless it is established that
  the act or omission was not reasonable in the circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity --- providing assistance

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A person who provides assistance or advice in
  taking or refraining from taking any measure under section 180 is not
  personally liable, either civilly or criminally, for anything that
  they do or omit to do in the course of providing the assistance or
  advice, unless it is established that the act or omission was not
  reasonable in the circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity --- accompanying Minister or
  officer

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} A person who accompanies the Minister of Fisheries
  and Oceans or a pollution response officer under subsection 180.1(2)
  or (3) is not personally liable, either civilly or criminally, for
  anything that they do or omit to do in the exercise of their powers
  under that subsection in accompanying the Minister or officer, unless
  it is established that the act or omission was not reasonable in the
  circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Civil or criminal liability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Response organizations, their agents or mandataries,
  and persons who have been designated in writing by the Minister as
  approved responders, are not personally liable, either civilly or
  criminally, in respect of any act or omission occurring or arising
  during the course of a response operation unless it is shown that the
  act or omission was committed with the intent to cause loss or damage,
  or recklessly and with the knowledge that loss or damage would
  probably result.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (1) affects the liability of
  the owner of a vessel, or of the vessel, that had discharged, was
  discharging or may have discharged a pollutant in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the occurrence that resulted in the taking of the
    measures referred to in subsection 180(1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any act or omission in the course of complying with
    a direction given under paragraph 180(1)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[response
  operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[response
  operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the activities undertaken
  following a discharge, or a grave and imminent threat of a discharge,
  from a vessel or an oil handling facility, including activities
  related to or connected with surveillance of and assessing areas of
  pollution, mobilizing and demobilizing response equipment and
  resources, protective booming, containment, recovery, dispersal or
  destruction of the pollutant, shoreline mitigation and restoration,
  transporting and disposing of recovered pollutant or waste materials
  and planning and supervising activities related to the response
  operation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 181
- 2014, c. 29, s. 68
- 2018, c. 27, s. 704
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-181-20141209.html "Link to previous version of section 181"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity --- civil liability

**[[181.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181.1 .sectionLabel}** The following
persons are not civilly liable for anything they do or omit to do in
good faith under this Part:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a [[servant]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} of the
  [[Crown]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those terms are defined in section
  2 of the [Crown Liability and Proceedings Act](/eng/acts/C-50); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a pollution response officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 27, s. 705
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52694 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[182]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-182 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make
  regulations for carrying out the purposes and provisions of this Part,
  including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which operators of
    oil handling facilities shall report discharges or anticipated
    discharges of pollutants, the manner of making the reports and the
    persons to whom the reports shall be made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the issuance, amendment, suspension,
    reinstatement, cancellation or renewal of certificates referred to
    in section 169;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the purposes for which fees may be
    charged in relation to an arrangement referred to in paragraph
    167(1)(a) or 168(1)(a) and the manner in which response
    organizations and persons who make an application under subsection
    169(1) must calculate proposed fees before notifying the Minister of
    the fees under subsection 170(1);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the establishment by response
    organizations of committees of persons who have arrangements with
    them and the provision to the committees of information regarding
    fees and proposed fees;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} establishing classes of oil handling facilities
    and determining which of the requirements set out in sections 167.1
    to 168.01 apply to the operators of, or to persons who propose to
    operate, oil handling facilities of each class;

  - [(d.2)]{.lawlabel} respecting oil pollution prevention plans and oil
    pollution emergency plans, including the time within which the plans
    shall be submitted to the Minister and the circumstances in which
    up-to-date plans shall be submitted to the Minister;

  - [(d.3)]{.lawlabel} respecting the procedures, equipment and
    resources referred to in paragraph 168(1)(e) and section 168.3;

  - [(d.4)]{.lawlabel} respecting the information and documents referred
    to in sections 167.1 and 167.3 and subsection 168.01(2), including
    the time within which the information and documents shall be
    submitted to the Minister; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that by this Part is to be
    prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite the regulations, the Minister may designate
  an oil handling facility that is part of a class established by the
  regulations to be part of a different class established by the
  regulations or an oil handling facility that is not part of a class
  established by the regulations to be part of one of those classes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall notify the operator of an oil
  handling facility of any designation made in respect of it under
  subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 182
- [2014, c. 29, s. 69](#1035591-1185980){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1035591-1185980.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-182-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 182"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52702 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act

- **[[183]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-183
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that,
  contravenes any of the following commits an offence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 167(1)(a) (have an arrangement);

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} subsection 167.2(1) (submission of oil pollution
    prevention plan and oil pollution emergency plan);

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} subsection 167.2(3) (prohibition against
    beginning operations);

  - [(a.3)]{.lawlabel} section 167.4 (submission of plans);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(1)(a) (have an arrangement);

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(1)(c.1) (submission of up-to-date
    oil pollution prevention plan);

  - [(b.2)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(1)(d.1) (submission of up-to-date
    oil pollution emergency plan);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(1)(e) (have procedures, equipment and
    resources available for immediate use);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(3)(a) (implement oil pollution
    prevention plan);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(3)(b) (implement oil pollution
    emergency plan);

  - [(e.01)]{.lawlabel} subsection 168.01(3) (revise plans);

  - [(e.02)]{.lawlabel} subsection 168.01(4) (prohibition against making
    changes);

  - [(e.1)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under section 168.1 (to update
    or revise plans);

  - [(e.2)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 168.3(b) (to
    take measures);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 171(b) (have equipment and resources at
    the site);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 171(e) (implement response plan);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 175.1(2)(a), (c)
    or (d) (direction resulting from a discharge or possible discharge
    of a pollutant);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 177(7) (giving clearance to detained
    vessel);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} subsection 177(8) (moving detained vessel);

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} section 178 (wilfully interfering with service of
    notice); or

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 180(1)(c) (to
    take measures or refrain from doing so).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that, commits an offence
  under subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not
  more than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than
  eighteen months, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 183
- 2005, c. 29, s. 28
- 2014, c. 29, s. 70
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-183-20141209.html "Link to previous version of section 183"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[184]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-184
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that,
  contravenes any of the following commits an offence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 167(1)(b) (have a declaration on board);

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} section 167.1 (notification of proposed
    operations);

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} subsection 167.2(2) (submission of information or
    documents);

  - [(a.3)]{.lawlabel} section 167.3 (notification of operations);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(1)(b) (have a declaration on site);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(1)(c) (have oil pollution prevention
    plan on site);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 168(1)(d) (have oil pollution emergency
    plan on site);

  - [(d.01)]{.lawlabel} subsection 168.01(1) (notification of proposed
    change to operations);

  - [(d.02)]{.lawlabel} subsection 168.01(2) (submission of information
    or documents);

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under section 168.2 (to provide
    information);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 171(a) (have a response plan);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 171(c) (provide or arrange for training);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 171(d) (undertake and participate in
    activities to evaluate response plan);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 171(f) (provide information);

  - [(h.1)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under section 171.1 (to provide
    documents);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 175.1(1)(a) (to
    provide information officer considers appropriate);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 175.1(1)(b) (to
    proceed by a route and not in excess of a speed);

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 175.1(1)(c) (to
    provide information relating to pollution plan);

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 175.1(1)(d)
    or (e) (to provide documents);

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 176(1)(b) (to
    provide reasonable assistance);

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 176(1)(c) or (d)
    (to provide information or to produce documents for inspection); and

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under this
    Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that, commits an offence
  under subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not
  more than \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than one
  year, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 184
- 2014, c. 29, s. 71
:::

## [PART 9]{.HLabel1}[Pollution Prevention --- Department of Transport]{.HTitleText1} {#h-52748 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52749 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[185]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-185 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[discharge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[discharge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a discharge of a pollutant
    that directly or indirectly results in the pollutant entering
    waters, and includes spilling, leaking, pumping, pouring, emitting,
    emptying, throwing and dumping. ([rejet]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[hazardous and noxious substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazardous and noxious substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    substance other than oil that, if introduced into the marine
    environment, is likely to create hazards to human health, harm
    living resources and marine life, damage amenities or interfere with
    legitimate uses of the marine environment. ([substance nocive et
    potentiellement dangereuse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hazardous and noxious substances pollution incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazardous and noxious substances pollution
    incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any occurrence or series of
    occurrences having the same origin, including fire or explosion,
    that results or may result in a discharge of hazardous and noxious
    substances. ([événement de pollution par les substances nocives et
    potentiellement dangereuses]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
    Transport. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[oil pollution incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[oil pollution incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an occurrence,
    or a series of occurrences having the same origin, that results or
    may result in a discharge of oil. ([événement de pollution par les
    hydrocarbures]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pollutant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pollutant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a substance that, if added to any waters, would
      degrade or alter or form part of a process of degradation or
      alteration of the quality of the waters to an extent that is
      detrimental to their use by humans or by an animal or a plant that
      is useful to humans; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any water that contains a substance in such a
      quantity or concentration, or that has been so treated, processed
      or changed, by heat or other means, from a natural state, that it
      would, if added to any waters, degrade or alter or form part of a
      process of degradation or alteration of the quality of the waters
      to an extent that is detrimental to their use by humans or by an
      animal or a plant that is useful to humans.

    It includes oil, hazardous and noxious substances and any substance
    or class of substances that is prescribed for the purpose of Part 8
    (Pollution Prevention and Response --- Department of Transport and
    Department of Fisheries and Oceans) to be a
    pollutant. ([polluant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 185
- 2005, c. 29, s. 29
- 2018, c. 27, s. 706
- [2023, c. 26, s. 399](#1410421-1413876){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410421-1413876.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-185-20181213.html "Link to previous version of section 185"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52761 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[186]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), this Part
  applies in respect of vessels in Canadian waters or waters in the
  exclusive economic zone of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply in respect of a discharge of
  oil or gas from a vessel that is on location and engaged in the
  exploration or drilling for, or the production, conservation or
  processing of, oil or gas in an area described in paragraph 3(a)
  or (b) of the [Canada Oil and Gas Operations Act](/eng/acts/O-7), in
  so far as the discharge emanates from those activities.

- Definition of [[oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
  [[gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (2), [[oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
  [[gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meaning as in section 2 of
  the [Canada Oil and Gas Operations Act](/eng/acts/O-7).

### [Authorized Representatives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1413877 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General duties

- **[[186.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative of a
  Canadian vessel must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the vessel and its machinery and
    equipment meet the requirements of the regulations made under this
    Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} develop procedures for preventing the discharge of
    a pollutant and for dealing with emergencies;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the crew receives training in emergency
    procedures; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the crew and passengers receive
    training in environmental protection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties --- Canadian maritime documents
  inspection

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The authorized representative of a Canadian vessel
  must ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vessel and its machinery and equipment are
    inspected for the purpose of obtaining all of the Canadian maritime
    documents that are required under this Part; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every term or condition attached to a Canadian
    maritime document issued in respect of the vessel or its machinery
    or equipment is met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 400](#1410426-1413890){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410426-1413890.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Masters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1413891 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Masters obtaining Canadian maritime
documents

**[[186.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186.2 .sectionLabel}** The master of a
Canadian vessel must, before the vessel embarks on a voyage from a port
in Canada, ensure that all of the Canadian maritime documents required
under this Part have been obtained.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 400](#1410426-1413895){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410426-1413895.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection of marine environment

- **[[186.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The master of a vessel must take all
  reasonable measures to ensure the protection of the marine
  environment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to take reasonable measures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the master of a vessel is informed that the vessel
  may have discharged, has discharged, is discharging or may discharge a
  pollutant, the master must take reasonable measures to protect the
  marine environment from the discharge or risk of discharge, including
  eliminating the risk if feasible. The master of a Canadian vessel must
  notify the authorized representative of the discharge or risk, unless
  the risk has been eliminated.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 400](#1410426-1413902){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410426-1413902.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Pollution Incidents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52769 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discharge of pollutant prohibited

**[[187]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187 .sectionLabel}** No person or vessel
shall discharge a prescribed pollutant, except in accordance with the
regulations made under this Part or a permit granted under Division 3 of
Part 7 of the [Canadian Environmental Protection Act,
1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Implementation of oil pollution emergency
plan

**[[188]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188 .sectionLabel}** If a vessel is required
by the regulations to have a shipboard oil pollution emergency plan, the
vessel shall take reasonable measures to implement the plan in respect
of an oil pollution incident.

### [Directions to Vessels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52774 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers in case of discharge

- **[[189]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Minister believes on reasonable grounds that a vessel may
  discharge, or may have discharged, a prescribed pollutant, the
  Minister may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} direct a vessel, if it is about to enter or is
    within waters to which this Part applies, to provide the Minister
    with any information that the Minister considers appropriate for the
    administration of this Part;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} direct a vessel that is required to have a
    shipboard oil pollution emergency plan under the regulations to
    provide him or her with any information concerning it and its
    implementation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct a vessel that is required to have on board a
    declaration described in paragraph 167(1)(b) to provide information
    concerning it;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} direct a vessel that is within or about to enter
    waters in respect of which this Part applies to proceed through
    those waters by the route and in the manner that the Minister may
    specify; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} direct the vessel to proceed to the place that the
    Minister may select, by the route and in the manner that the
    Minister may specify, and to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} unload the pollutant, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} moor, anchor or remain there for any reasonable
      period that the Minister may specify.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to authorize vessel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may direct a port authority or a person
  in charge of a port authority or place to authorize a vessel in
  respect of which a direction has been made under paragraph (1)(d) to
  proceed to the place selected by the Minister and to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unload the pollutant; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} moor, anchor or remain there for any reasonable
    period that the Minister may specify.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 189
- 2005, c. 29, s. 30
- [2023, c. 26, s. 402](#1410430-1413922){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410430-1413922.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-189-20051005.html "Link to previous version of section 189"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52786 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[190]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-190 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make
  regulations respecting the protection of the marine environment,
  including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing pollutants for the purpose of section
    187 and subsection 189(1) and respecting the circumstances in which
    such pollutants may be discharged;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which persons on
    board vessels shall report discharges or anticipated discharges, the
    manner of making the reports and the persons to whom the reports
    shall be made;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the carrying of pollutants on board a
    vessel, whether as cargo or fuel;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the control and prevention of pollution
    of the air by vessels;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting reception facilities for oily residues,
    chemical residues, garbage and sewage;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the control and management of ballast
    water;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for preventing or reducing the release by vessels
    into waters of aquatic organisms or pathogens that, if released into
    those waters, could create hazards to human health, harm organisms,
    damage amenities, impair biological diversity or interfere with
    legitimate uses of the waters;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting the design, construction, manufacture
    and maintenance of vessels or classes of vessels;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} specifying the machinery, equipment and supplies
    that must be on board vessels or classes of vessels;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} respecting the design, construction, manufacture,
    maintenance, storage, testing, arrangement and use of vessels' or
    classes of vessels' machinery, equipment and supplies;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} respecting the requirements that vessels, or
    classes of vessels, and their machinery and equipment must meet;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} requiring the obtaining of certificates certifying
    that any of the requirements referred to in paragraph (k) are met;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} respecting the inspection and testing of vessels,
    or classes of vessels, and their machinery, equipment and supplies;
    and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} respecting shipboard hazardous and noxious
    substances pollution emergency plans.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under subsection (1) apply in
  respect of vessels that are capable of engaging in the drilling for,
  or the production, conservation or processing of, oil or gas only if
  the regulations so state and were made on the joint recommendation of
  the Minister and the Minister of Natural Resources.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 190
- 2018, c. 27, s. 707(E)
- [2023, c. 26, s. 403](#1410438-1413927){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410438-1413927.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-190-20181213.html "Link to previous version of section 190"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-52807 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[191]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-191
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, or vessel that,
  contravenes any of the following commits an offence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 186.1(1)(a) (ensure vessel meets
    requirements);

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 186.1(1)(b) (develop procedures);

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 186.1(1)(c) (ensure emergency
    procedures training);

  - [(a.3)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 186.1(1)(d) (ensure environmental
    protection training);

  - [(a.4)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 186.1(2)(a) (ensure vessel inspected);

  - [(a.5)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 186.1(2)(b) (ensure terms and
    conditions met);

  - [(a.6)]{.lawlabel} section 186.2 (obtain Canadian maritime
    documents);

  - [(a.7)]{.lawlabel} subsection 186.3(1) (protection of marine
    environment);

  - [(a.8)]{.lawlabel} subsection 186.3(2) (take reasonable measures);

  - [(a.9)]{.lawlabel} section 187 (discharge of a pollutant);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 188 (implement shipboard oil pollution
    emergency plan);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subparagraph 189(1)(d)(i)
    (direction to proceed to a place and unload a pollutant);

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subsection 189(2)
    (direction to authorize a vessel); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a provision of the regulations made under this
    Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person or vessel that commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is liable on summary conviction to a fine of not more
  than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than 18
  months, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing offence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an offence under paragraph (1)(a) is committed or
  continued on more than one day, the person or vessel that committed it
  is liable to be convicted for a separate offence for each day on which
  it is committed or continued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors to be considered

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In determining the punishment under subsection (2),
  the court may have regard to the following factors:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the harm or risk of harm caused by the offence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an estimate of the total costs of clean-up, of harm
    caused, and of the best available mitigation measures;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the remedial action taken, or proposed to be taken,
    by the offender to mitigate the harm;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} whether the discharge or anticipated discharge was
    reported in accordance with the regulations made under paragraph
    190(1)(b);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any economic benefits accruing to the offender
    that, but for the offence, the offender would not have received; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any evidence from which the court may reasonably
    conclude that the offender has a history of non-compliance with
    legislation designed to prevent or to minimize pollution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 26, s. 191
- [2023, c. 26, s. 404](#1410443-1413942){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410443-1413942.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-191-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 191"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of directions

- **[[192]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-192
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every vessel commits an offence that
  contravenes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 189(1)(a) or (b)
    (direction to provide information);

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 189(1)(a.1)
    (direction to provide information --- shipboard oil pollution
    emergency plan);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under paragraph 189(1)(c)
    (direction to proceed by a specified route); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subparagraph 189(1)(d)(ii)
    (direction to proceed to a place and remain there).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every vessel that commits an offence under
